The proposed approach uses vibration data (displacement and velocity measurements), domain knowledge of modal damping ratios, and a physics-based model that can yield an approximate natural frequencies time-dependency model. The approach is validated using synthetic data generated from a finite element model of a 2-blade offshore wind turbine under realistic environmental and operating conditions. This system displays fast time-varying frequencies due to operating conditions, whose identification is particularly challenging because of the wind and wave loading. The robustness of the proposed approach is assessed under assumed incorrect system information (e.g. damping ratio). The proposed approach is evaluated across different environmental and operating conditions to show its applicability to different operating regimes. The results show the approach can accurately identify the selected fast-varying natural frequency, 1st Fore-Aft (FA-1) mode, with a maximum root mean square error of 0.0012 Hz. The results demonstrate that the model trained on EKF estimates depends on accurate damping values, whereas the model trained on physics-based data exhibits robustness to incorrect damping assumptions. The approach is extended to damping ratio identification for the selected mode by estimating the root mean square error between models trained on EKF estimates and physics-based data. The results show that the approach can yield a good approximation of the FA-1 mode damping ratio using grid search, offering an improvement over covariance-driven stochastic subspace identification.

</details>


### [331] [QUALS: Corpus Equilibrium for Universal Forecasting via Pattern Quantization and Learnability Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20156)
*Yujie Li,Zezhi Shao,Chengqing Yu,Yisong Fu,Weijie Zhu,Yifan Du,Jilin Hu,Bin Yang,Yongjun Xu,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QUALS是一个大规模时间序列语料均衡框架，通过模式量化和可学习性同步机制提升数据效率，使现有模型仅用少量数据即可获得更优的零样本预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列基础模型研究偏重架构创新，忽视数据多样性，常采用简单采样策略，难以应对复杂数据分布，导致训练数据利用低效、性能次优。

Method: QUALS包含两大机制：一是模式量化框架，通过向量量化和均匀分箱从混合语料中系统解码异构模式；二是可学习性同步框架，校准异构模式的采样权重，弥合简单与复杂模式间的优化差距。

Result: 大量基准测试表明，在QUALS上预训练即使大幅缩减训练预算，也能持续取得更优的零样本性能。

Conclusion: QUALS通过语料均衡显著提升数据效率，为时间序列基础模型的高效预训练提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Ubiquitous time series data across diverse domains enables critical applications in areas such as transportation systems and power grids. Recently, training foundation models on massive datasets to achieve accurate zero-shot forecasting has emerged as a major research focus. However, current studies predominantly prioritize architectural innovations while insufficiently addressing data diversity, often relying on simple data sampling strategies that fail to manage complex data distributions effectively, leading to inefficient use of training data and suboptimal performance. To address this, we propose QUALS, a large-scale time series corpus equilibrium framework. QUALS significantly enhances data efficiency, i.e., enabling existing models to achieve superior performance using only a small fraction of the original training data. Specifically, QUALS operates through two core mechanisms. First, a pattern quantization framework systematically decodes heterogeneous patterns from mixed corpora via vector quantization and uniform binning. Second, a learnability synchronization framework calibrates sampling weights for heterogeneous patterns, bridging the optimization gap between simple and complex motifs to maximize overall training efficiency. Extensive benchmarks demonstrate that pre-training on QUALS consistently achieves superior zero-shot performance, even under substantially reduced training budgets.

</details>


### [332] [A Noise Optimum in Rehearsal-Free Continual Learning: Isolation, Mechanism, and Scope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20162)
*Gunner Levi Howe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该仿真研究揭示了持续学习中注入随机噪声与任务保持之间的倒U型关系，其根源在于向已固化权重的"相干恢复力"与噪声方差的耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望厘清为何向固化规则注入随机噪声会在某最优点前改善早期任务保持、之后又使其退化（保持率-噪声呈倒U型），并界定该现象成立的边界条件。

Method: (1) 在Split-MNIST、FashionMNIST、持续Yin-Yang等基准上复现倒U现象；(2) 用幅度匹配的"阶梯"实验分离出相干恢复力的作用；(3) 将锚定增益与噪声方差σ²耦合（一行的规则），并用受迫Ornstein-Uhlenbeck计算推导上升侧；(4) 通过旋转vs置换MNIST对比定位任务结构边界；(5) 在不同任务数下检验长度效应及紧凑群恒等式。(6) 另有单种子BrainScaleS-2演示（Howe, arXiv:2607.06924）。

Result: 倒U型保持现象在多个相关任务基准上出现；仅相干恢复力能产生最优，随机方向力无效，向错误目标恢复反而有害；增益-噪声方差耦合可恢复大部分最优，且推导预测最优噪声随任务干扰g上升（方向与既有测量一致）；置换MNIST上无最优，边界定位到共享任务结构；优势随任务数增加而衰减，无旋转族可解释该趋势。

Conclusion: 保持率-噪声倒U型最优源于将锚定增益耦合到注入噪声方差，需要任务共享结构，且随任务数增加而衰减；精确控制量仍待明确，硬件声明归入他文。

Abstract: Injecting stochastic noise into a consolidation rule can improve a network's retention of earlier tasks up to an optimal level, then degrade it -- an inverted-U in retention vs. noise. This paper isolates what produces that optimum and maps where it holds, entirely in simulation. (1) Phenomenon: the retention inverted-U appears on several related-task continual-learning benchmarks (Split-MNIST, FashionMNIST, continual Yin-Yang). (2) Isolation: a magnitude-matched ladder shows the effect requires coherent restoring toward the consolidated weights -- a random-direction force of identical magnitude produces no optimum, and a coherent force toward the wrong target actively hurts. (3) Active ingredient: most of the optimum is recovered by coupling the anchor gain to the injected-noise variance sigma^2 -- a one-line rule that neither Ornstein-Uhlenbeck Adaptation (fixed gain) nor MESU (posterior-variance gain) implements. A forced Ornstein-Uhlenbeck calculation derives the rising flank and predicts that the optimal noise rises with per-task interference g -- confirmed out-of-sample in direction against pre-existing measurements (the exponent is unresolved at our grid). The barrier-conditioning of the originating Doob h-transform is a low-sigma safety net that bounds forgetting where the coupled gain is too weak. (4) Scope: the optimum requires shared task structure -- it is absent on permuted-MNIST, and a controlled rotated-vs-permuted comparison localizes the boundary to task structure; the precise governing quantity is left open. (5) Length: at matched severity the advantage persists but attenuates with task count, and we show no rotation family can attribute the trend (a compact-group identity). A single-seed BrainScaleS-2 demonstration of the originating rule is reported separately (Howe, arXiv:2607.06924); this paper makes no hardware claim.

</details>


### [333] [Small Enough to Know Everything: The Fully-Enumerable Transformer as an Instrument for the Science of Delayed Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20166)
*Yoshiyuki Ootani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 小型可枚举任务上的Transformer作为研究grokking的"科学仪器"，其任务侧规律在12K到50M参数间大部分保守，一项规律随规模系统性变形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可枚举任务具备可精确评估、可计算泛化上限、低成本多随机种子等优势，但存在质疑：在10^4参数量级上刻画的规律是否可外推到更大规模。本文旨在检验这些规律的规模可迁移性。

Method: 提出可枚举transformer的四种能力（精确可证伪的泛化上限、任务手术、全权重视察、生存时间统计）。通过预注册的保守性研究，将12K参数下建立的三条任务侧规律（可恢复性上限律、角色冲突延迟律、权重衰减响应律）在12K、1M、50M参数（4000倍跨度，360次运行+44次对照）的相同从头训练协议下重新测量。

Result: 上限律与延迟律得到保守（144次Holm校正上限违反0次；各规模Spearman rho>=0.75，置换p<1e-4）；权重衰减律系统性变形，随规模增大而变陡。50M角色冲突缺陷在调整学习率和三倍预算后仍存在。

Conclusion: 保守性检验标准在数据收集前冻结，且存在失败可能（一项规律变形即证明）。可枚举transformer可作为延迟泛化任务侧规律的模型生物：它精确测量的规律，大模型大体遵循；偏离之处本身也规律且可测量。

Abstract: Tiny transformers trained on fully-enumerable tasks occupy an unusual position in the study of grokking: every input can be evaluated, every generalization ceiling can be computed exactly, and hundreds of seeds cost minutes. We argue this regime is a scientific instrument with four capabilities that approximate settings cannot offer: (a) exact, falsifiable generalization ceilings; (b) task surgery that manipulates one structural variable while provably fixing all others; (c) direct observation of every weight; and (d) survival-time statistics over many seeds that recast "does not grok" as a censored observation. The obvious objection is that laws characterized at 10^4 parameters may not mean anything beyond them. We answer it with a preregistered conservation study: three task-side laws established at 12K parameters -- a recoverability-ceiling law, a role-conflict delay law, and a weight-decay response law -- are re-measured under an identical from-scratch protocol at 12K, 1M, and 50M parameters (a 4,000x span; 360 runs plus a 44-run control arm). The ceiling law and the delay law are conserved (0/144 Holm-corrected ceiling violations; Spearman rho >= 0.75 at every scale, permutation p < 1e-4), while the weight-decay law deforms systematically, steepening with scale. Preregistered controls show the 50M role-conflict deficit survives learning-rate adjustment and a tripled budget. Conservation was tested against criteria frozen before data collection, and one law's deformation shows the test could have failed. These results license the fully-enumerable transformer as a model organism for the task-side laws of delayed generalization: what it measures exactly, larger models largely obey -- and where they deviate, the deviation is itself lawful and measurable.

</details>


### [334] [Support Thresholds, Not Algorithms, Limit Rare-Association Recovery in Co-Purchase Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20171)
*Xiao Han,Zhen Zhang,Xin Zhao,Jiechun Lei,Moxuan Zheng,Youting Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较Apriori、lift后过滤、top-K及两种网络方法(NC、DF)在购物篮共购边过滤上的表现，NC和top-K显著优于Apriori，且各有偏向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apriori的支持度阈值存在权衡：高阈值遗漏频繁关联，低阈值产生大量规则。需要更好的共购边过滤方法。

Method: 在Instacart(320万篮)和Dunnhumby(20.8万篮)上比较五种方法：Apriori、Apriori+lift后过滤、基于lift的top-K、网络噪声校正(NC)和差异过滤(DF)。并用滚动原点留出法评估边稳定性。

Result: top-K平均lift最高，NC以单一显著性参数α达到相近lift。两者比Apriori恢复更多稀有高lift关联(80-100% vs 22-28%)；NC和top-K选取的边有18-29%不重叠。滚动评估中top-K边复现率更高，但NC边在留出网络中保持统计显著的概率高约12个百分点。

Conclusion: NC与top-K优于Apriori且选择不同：NC保留统计验证对，top-K保留高lift稀有对。方法选择取决于对统计显著性与边复现性的偏好。

Abstract: The support threshold of the Apriori algorithm involves a trade-off in conducting market basket analysis: the associations that occur frequently are noted with high threshold; however, the low ones lead to generating the large amount of rules. The paper compares five methods for co-purchase edge filtration on two grocery datasets: i.e., Instacart (3.2 million baskets) and Dunnhumby (208 thousand baskets), including Apriori, Apriori + lift post-filtering, top-$K$ ranking based on lift, and two methods based on networks, noise-corrected (NC) and disparity filter (DF). The top-$K$ method ensures the maximum average lift, while the NC achieves similar lift level by means of a single value of the significance parameter ($α$). These two methods recover substantially more rare high-lift associations than Apriori (80-100% against 22-28%). NC and top-$K$ select meaningfully different edges (18-29% non-overlapping): NC retains statistically validated pairs, while top-$K$ retains rare pairs with high lift but low statistical significance. A rolling-origin holdout evaluation shows that top-$K$ edges recur at higher rates at every split, but NC edges are ~12 pp more likely to remain statistically significant in the held-out network.

</details>


### [335] [Robust Federated Q-Learning with Almost No Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20174)
*Sreejeet Maity,Aritra Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在存在少量恶意代理的联邦强化学习场景下，提出Robust Fed-Q算法，结合模型基与无模型RL及中位数均值方法，实现最优价值函数的精确收敛和接近最优的协作加速。且每次仅需$\tilde{O}(1)$通信轮数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在联邦强化学习中，当少量代理为对抗性（恶意）时，协作采样是否能带来样本复杂度收益，以及如何应对这种鲁棒性挑战。

Method: 提出Robust Fed-Q算法，融合模型基与无模型Q学习思想，并采用鲁棒统计中的中位数均值（median-of-means）技术来抵抗恶意代理的任意行为。

Result: 证明Robust Fed-Q在存在腐败的情况下：(i) 当样本量趋于无穷时，以高概率精确收敛到最优价值函数；(ii) 在有限时间内获得接近最优的协作加速率。此外，每次保证仅需$\tilde{O}(1)$轮通信。

Conclusion: Robust Fed-Q能在有恶意代理的联邦RL中实现鲁棒且高效的协作学习，同时显著降低通信开销，对通信瓶颈突出的联邦学习有意义。

Abstract: We consider a federated reinforcement learning setting involving $M$ agents, all of whom interact with a common Markov Decision Process (MDP). The agents exchange information via a central server to learn the optimal value function. Our goal is to understand to what extent one can hope for collaborative sample-complexity speedups in such a setting, when a small fraction of the agents are adversarial and can act arbitrarily. To that end, we propose Robust Fed-Q}, a federated Q-learning algorithm that blends ideas from both model-based and model-free RL, along with the median-of-means device from robust statistics. We prove that despite corruption, with high-probability, Robust Fed-Q (i) guarantees exact convergence to the optimal value function in the limit of infinite samples, and (ii) enjoys near-optimal finite-time rates that benefit from collaboration. In addition, our approach requires just $\tilde{O}(1)$ rounds of communication to achieve each of the above guarantees, a feature of independent interest in FL where communication is the major bottleneck.

</details>


### [336] [When Does Retrieval Help Time-Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20193)
*Mert Onur Cakiroglu,Elham Buxton,Mehmet Dalkilic,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 检索插件带来的增益其实来自运行点（窗口长度S与季节周期L的关系），而非插件机制本身；标准协议从未改变这一维度。分层评估暴露了该机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已发表评估普遍报告检索插件带来一致增益，并各自归因于自身机制，但这些评估沿用固定协议，从未改变窗口长度与季节周期的关系这一关键维度。

Method: 按窗口长度S与主要季节周期L的关系进行分层评估；用"重复上一个观测周期"的简单对照与六个标准骨干、最强插件对比；控制合成扫描horizon、period、window；配对对照去除相位；零样本预训练测试；两个可解释统计量（趋势检验、陈旧率）。

Result: S=12时简单对照在四个基准上以8%–44% MSE胜过六个骨干，在ETTm1上胜最强插件、在ECL上持平；在三个训练谱无集中共享周期的数据集上最多差25%。增益边界随周期（相关+0.71）而非horizon（−0.23）。无相位对照几乎消除效应，符合相位饥饿。基础模型在周期基准上落后22%–50%。精确查找与图扩散相当。两统计量在留一数据集评估下以0.76准确率预测增益符号，高于多数规则0.69。

Conclusion: 增益源自"查阅记录"本身而非其上机制；贡献是揭示该机制的制度图、评估协议及可在部署前筛选的两个统计量，而非新插件。

Abstract: Retrieval plug-ins supply a deep forecaster with information its lookback window cannot carry. Published evaluations report consistent gains, and each credits its own mechanism. We show that the benefit belongs instead to the operating point: the relation between window length $S$ and dominant seasonal period $L$, an axis the standard protocol never varies. Stratifying the evaluation by that relation exposes the regime. At $S{=}12$, a simple control that repeats the last observed period beats the six standard backbones, in aggregate, on four of seven benchmarks by $8\%$ to $44\%$ of MSE. It beats the strongest plug-in we run on ETTm1 and matches it on ECL. It is worse by up to $25\%$ on the three datasets whose training-split spectra lack a concentrated, shared period. A controlled synthetic sweep of horizon, period, and window shows the benefit boundary tracks the period (correlation $+0.71$), not the horizon ($-0.23$). A paired control with no phase to recover nearly erases the effect, consistent with phase starvation. Zero-shot pretraining does not escape it: a foundation model trails trained backbones by $22\%$ to $50\%$ on the periodic benchmarks. Within our instrument, exact lookup matches graph diffusion: the payoff is consulting the record, not the machinery on top. Two interpretable statistics, a trend test and a staleness rate, predict the sign of the per-cell benefit at $0.76$ accuracy under leave-one-dataset-out evaluation, a suggestive margin over the $0.69$ majority rule, where a 22-feature stack manages $0.57$. We propose no new plug-in. The contribution is the regime map, the protocol that reveals it, and two statistics that screen it before deployment. Code: https://github.com/KurbanIntelligenceLab/retrieval-regime.

</details>


### [337] [SoftTri: Smooth Triangular Membership Functions for Adaptive Fuzzy Inference Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20194)
*Babak Sarani,Rahman Ardakanian,Ali Mousavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可微三角隶属函数SoftTri，解决传统三角MF不可导问题，提升神经模糊系统优化稳定性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统三角MF因可解释性、低参数复杂度和强局部性被广泛使用，但在节点处不可导，限制梯度优化，常需次梯度或启发式平滑。

Method: 基于Swish类激活的软铰链机制构建SoftTri，保持三角几何与局部性，对输入及参数(a,b,c)实现C∞光滑，并给出闭式解析梯度，集成到网格分区的Takagi-Sugeno模糊神经网络。

Result: 在多个1D/2D非线性逼近基准和Airfoil Self-Noise回归任务上，SoftTri比经典三角MF优化更稳定、精度更高，性能与高斯MF相当或更优。

Conclusion: SoftTri在可解释性与可微软优化之间取得有效平衡，适用于现代神经模糊学习系统。

Abstract: Triangular membership functions (MFs) are widely used in fuzzy systems because of their interpretability, low parameterization complexity, and strong locality properties. However, their inherent nondifferentiability at knot points limits the effectiveness of gradient-based optimization in adaptive neuro-fuzzy architectures, often necessitating subgradient approximations or heuristic smoothing techniques. In this paper, we propose \emph{SoftTri}, a differentiable triangular membership function constructed using a smooth soft-hinge mechanism inspired by Swish-type activations. The proposed formulation preserves the geometric structure and localized behavior of classical triangular MFs while providing $C^\infty$ smoothness with respect to both the input variable and the membership parameters $(a,b,c)$ for any finite sharpness parameter $β>0$. Closed-form analytical gradients are derived to enable efficient and fully differentiable backpropagation-based learning. SoftTri is integrated into a Takagi--Sugeno fuzzy neural network with grid-partitioned rules and evaluated on multiple one-dimensional and two-dimensional nonlinear approximation benchmarks as well as a real-world regression task using the Airfoil Self-Noise dataset. Experimental results demonstrate that SoftTri consistently improves optimization stability and approximation accuracy compared with classical triangular membership functions, while achieving performance comparable to or better than Gaussian MFs under identical rule structures and training settings. The proposed approach provides an effective compromise between interpretability and differentiable optimization in modern neuro-fuzzy learning systems.

</details>


### [338] [Evaluating Financial Sentiment in the Age of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20198)
*Arslan Bisharat,Oudom Hean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估12种情感模型（词典、金融专用Transformer、开源LLM），从语言与经济效度两方面发现：通用LLM分类性能可媲美金融专用模型，但更高准确率未带来更强经济关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融情感度量在实证金融中广泛使用，但通用大语言模型是否优于现有金融专用方法尚不明确，需系统评估。

Method: 评估12种情感模型（词典法、金融专用Transformer、开源LLM），采用语言效度与经济效度双重标准，考察其与盈利意外及次日股票收益的关联。

Result: 通用LLM无需任务微调即可达到与金融专用Transformer相当的分类性能；但更高分类准确率并未转化为更强经济关系。多个模型的情感度量与盈利意外显著相关，但无一与次日股票收益显著相关。对大幅盈利超预期/不及预期的公告效果最强，温和意外时明显减弱。

Conclusion: 金融情感能捕捉企业经济表现信息，但解释短期市场反应的能力有限。

Abstract: Financial sentiment measures are widely used in empirical finance, but it remains unclear whether general-purpose large language models (LLMs) improve on existing finance-specific methods. This paper evaluates twelve sentiment models, including dictionary-based methods, finance-specific transformers, and open-source LLMs, using two criteria: linguistic validity and economic validity. We find that general-purpose LLMs achieve classification performance comparable to finance-specific transformer models without task-specific fine-tuning. However, higher classification accuracy does not translate into stronger economic relationships. Several models produce sentiment measures that are significantly associated with earnings surprises, but none is significantly associated with next-day stock returns. Model performance is strongest for announcements with large earnings beats or misses and substantially weaker for announcements with more moderate earnings surprises. These findings suggest that financial sentiment captures information about firms' economic performance but has limited ability to explain short-run market reactions

</details>


### [339] [Towards a Unified Modality-Agnostic Multimodal Framework for Cognitive Workload Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20199)
*Stefanos Gkikas,Christian Arzate Cruz,Calvin Joseph,Giorgos Giannakakis,Raul Fernandez Rojas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种统一、模态无关的层次化Transformer架构，用于处理异质生物信号，并在5种模态的31种组合中评估认知工作负荷测量效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知工作负荷测量对自适应人机系统设计至关重要，虽然生物信号测量已被广泛研究，但关于异质生物信号模态组合效果的研究仍然有限。

Method: 开发统一、模态无关的层次化Transformer架构，在单一模型中处理异质生物信号；采用留一被试验证，评估ECG、EDA、RESP、SpO₂、EEG五种模态全部31种组合，涵盖IQ、MATH、GAME三类认知任务。

Result: EEG为最强单模态，在IQ、GAME及合并ALL设置中排名最高；增加模态并不持续提升性能；完整五模态组合在IQ上达最高Average分数73.02%，四设置平均为68.08%；相比late-fusion方案，模型规模减小约50%且推理时间更低。

Conclusion: 单一模态（尤其EEG）表现突出，多模态组合并非总是更优；所提方法在保持较低推理时间的同时显著减小模型规模，为异质生物信号融合提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Cognitive workload reflects the mental effort required during task performance and is central to the design of adaptive human-machine systems. The use of biosignals to measure cognitive workload has been extensively researched and documented; however, studies examining the effects of combining heterogeneous biosignal modalities for this purpose remain limited. To provide insight into this area, we developed a unified, modality-agnostic, hierarchical Transformer-based architecture to process heterogeneous biosignal modalities within a single model. We use this framework in a pilot study evaluating all $31$ possible combinations of five modalities: Electrocardiogram (ECG), Electrodermal Activity (EDA), Respiration (RESP), Peripheral Oxygen Saturation (SpO$_2$), and Electroencephalogram (EEG), under leave-one-subject-out validation across three cognitively distinct tasks: abstract reasoning (IQ), arithmetic problem solving (MATH), and a game task (GAME). In this pilot setting, the results suggest that: (i) EEG is the strongest single modality, ranking highest in IQ, GAME, and the pooled ALL setting, where samples from all three tasks are combined; (ii) adding more modalities does not consistently improve performance; (iii) the full five-modality combination achieves the highest \textit{Average} score of $73.02%$ on IQ and $68.08%$ when the \textit{Average} scores are averaged over the four evaluation settings: IQ, MATH, GAME, and ALL; and (iv) the proposed method reduces model size by approximately $50%$ compared with late-fusion alternatives while maintaining a lower inference time.

</details>


### [340] [Scene-Conditioned Relation Routing for urban cellular activity forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20209)
*Qingzhong Li,Jingye Lin,Hui Ma,Yajun Zhang,Xinjun Pei,Ming Yan,Fei Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCRR-Net，通过场景条件化的空间关系路由框架统一建模城市多信号时空预测，在米兰和特伦托数据集上性能优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市蜂窝活动预测需联合建模多种异构时空信号（短信、网络流量、通话），但现有方法将时间建模、空间关系学习和多信号预测分离，依赖固定图结构或静态多任务学习，难以适应变化的城市场景。

Method: 提出场景条件化空间关系路由框架SCRR-Net，利用城市上下文信息同时控制空间依赖选择与跨任务知识迁移，包含上下文编码器、空间图专家路由模块、时间Transformer编码器和任务知识路由模块。

Result: 在Milano和Trento数据集上，SCRR-Net在短信、网络流量和通话活动预测任务上均持续优于竞争方法，并提供可解释的路由行为。

Conclusion: SCRR-Net通过场景条件化路由实现自适应空间关系与跨任务知识迁移，验证了其城市蜂窝活动预测的有效性与可解释性。

Abstract: Urban cellular activity forecasting requires jointly modeling heterogeneous spatiotemporal signals, including SMS usage, mobile network traffic, and call activity. Existing methods often separate temporal modeling, spatial relation learning, and multi-signal prediction, relying on fixed graph structures or static multi-task learning schemes, which limits their adaptability to changing urban scenes. We propose SCRR-Net, a scene-conditioned spatial relation routing framework in which urban contextual information jointly controls spatial dependency selection and cross-task knowledge transfer. SCRR-Net includes a context encoder, a spatial graph expert routing module, a temporal Transformer encoder, and a task knowledge routing module. Experiments on the Milano and Trento datasets demonstrate that SCRR-Net consistently outperforms competing methods on SMS, network traffic, and call activity forecasting, while providing interpretable routing behaviors.

</details>


### [341] [Subdomain-aware representation compression for pretrained image embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20213)
*Poowanut Niamluang,Jittat Fakcharoenphol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨在子域表示压缩中应用降维技术（PCA、LDA），在图像域中不仅提升空间与计算效率，还意外提高精度，并验证了压缩表示的迁移学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统降维通常对整个数据集统一应用，本文希望探索其在子域表示压缩中的潜力，尤其是面向边缘设备ML应用的空间与计算效率提升。

Method: 在图像域中应用标准降维技术，包括主成分分析（PCA）和线性判别分析（LDA），对子域表示进行压缩，并与直接使用完整嵌入的方法进行对比；同时开展迁移学习实验。

Result: 降维在空间和计算复杂度上取得预期改善，对边缘设备ML至关重要；同时相比直接使用完整嵌入的方法，精度得到提升，推测是降维有效提取了子域特征。

Conclusion: 降维技术可用于子域表示压缩，兼顾效率与精度，且压缩表示具备迁移学习能力。

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction is a well-known technique for improving space efficiency, typically applied uniformly across an entire dataset. This paper investigates the possibilities of using dimensionality reduction techniques for subdomain representation compression. We explore standard techniques such as Principal Component Analysis (PCA) and Linear discriminant analysis (LDA) in image domains. The results not only demonstrate the expected improvements in space and computation complexity crucial for edge-device ML applications but also show improvements in accuracy over direct full-embedding procedure. One possible explanation is that dimensionality reduction effectively extracts subdomain features. We also performed experiments to demonstrate transfer learning capabilities using the compressed representations.

</details>


### [342] [Explaining spatial information flow in short-term traffic forecasting models using a gated graph attention network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20217)
*Yue Li,Shujuan Chen,Ying Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过在GAT层加入可学习的门控并正则化，实现渐进式消融，揭示GAT层何时何处需要邻居信息，发现两层GAT大多冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GAT层被广泛描述为能捕捉邻居位置的影响，但因注意力权重缺乏可比的实测指标而难以验证，导致两个问题悬而未决：模型应如何解释，以及哪些组件是必要的。

Method: 在GAT层加入门控，学习每个传感器、每个时间步中更新状态来自邻居与自身的比例；对门控正则化以渐进撤回邻居信息，形成分级消融。将门控GAT应用于ST-MetaNet（编码器与解码器各在两层循环层之间放一层GAT），用英格兰战略路网498个环形检测器一年数据训练。

Result: 门控给流量更大的传感器分配更多邻居信息，并跟随出行日/周周期；轻度正则化略微提升精度，强正则化则因撤回必要信息而降低精度。编码器门控先于解码器关闭，但直接消融对这一顺序提出质疑：单独移除任一GAT层精度至少与保留两者相当，同时移除两层则显著下降。

Conclusion: 门控GAT带来小幅精度提升，给出空间信息流动位置与时机的解释，并提供架构层必要性的证据：两层GAT基本冗余，任一层都不是不可或缺的。

Abstract: Short-term traffic forecasting supports real-time monitoring and control of road networks, and graph attention networks (GAT) are the standard means of representing spatial dependence in these models. GAT layers are widely described as capturing the influence of neighbouring locations, but this is seldom verified, because the attention weights offered in support cannot be compared against any measured quantity. That leaves two questions open, how the model should be explained and which of its components are necessary. We address this by adding a gate to the GAT layer which learns, at every sensor and every time step, what share of a sensor's updated state is drawn from its neighbours rather than from itself. Regularising the gate withdraws neighbour information progressively and thereby provides a graded form of ablation. We apply the gated GAT to ST-MetaNet, whose encoder and decoder each place one GAT layer between two recurrent layers, and train it on one calendar year of records from 498 loop detectors on the strategic road network of England. The gate assigns a larger share of neighbour information to sensors carrying heavier traffic and follows the daily and weekly cycle of travel, consistent with adjacent locations being more strongly coupled when busy. Mild regularisation improves accuracy slightly, and accuracy declines at higher strengths as the penalty withdraws information the model needs. The encoder gate closes before the decoder gate, but direct ablation qualifies that ordering. Removing either GAT layer alone leaves accuracy at least as good as keeping both, whereas removing both degrades it substantially, so the two layers are largely redundant rather than either being indispensable. The gated GAT therefore yields a modest accuracy gain, an explanation of where and when spatial information flows, and evidence on which layers the architecture requires.

</details>


### [343] [Is It Still Worth Training a Classical Model in the Era of LLMs? A Crossover Benchmark on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20218)
*Kaihua Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，对典型业务表格预测问题，标注几百个样本训练梯度提升模型通常优于提示冻结的小型LLM；训练模型最快用约6%训练集规模即可超越LLM的零样本/提示误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可在无训练情况下对表格行进行标注并已进入Excel等主流工具，但在标签昂贵的业务预测中，是应提示冻结LLM还是收集数据训练模型、以及需要多少数据，缺乏定量答案。

Method: 提出“标注数据交叉点N*”指标：训练经典模型学习曲线超过冻结LLM平坦误差所需训练集大小。聚合18个表格数据集、126次独立学生评估、8种提示配置的小型GPT模型，并结合六类经典模型的幂律学习曲线；另测试少样本示例、独立复现差异及特征名语义依赖。

Result: 即使给LLM最优提示配置，训练经典模型在86%情况下用现有数据量即可获胜，40%情况下用最小标注子集获胜；交叉点中位数约为训练集6%。少样本示例误差与样本数不呈幂律；独立复现变异系数0.148。LLM依赖可识别特征名语义，使交叉点估计偏保守。

Conclusion: 对典型业务表格，证据明确支持收集几百个标签并训练梯度提升模型，而非依赖冻结LLM提示。

Abstract: Large language models can label a tabular row from a plain-English description with no training - a capability now shipping in mainstream spreadsheet tools such as Microsoft Copilot in Excel and Anthropic's Claude for Excel - raising a practical question for the many business prediction problems where labels are expensive: should you prompt a frozen LLM, or collect data and train a model - and if so, how much data? We quantify the answer with the labeled-data crossover N*, the training-set size at which a trained classical model's learning curve overtakes a frozen LLM's training-free (and therefore flat) error. Aggregating 126 independent student evaluations of small GPT models under eight prompting configurations across 18 tabular datasets, paired with authoritative power-law learning curves for six classical model families, we find that training wins fast: even given an oracle choice of its best prompt configuration, a trained classical model beats the small frozen LLM using no more labeled data than is already on hand in 86% of cases, and wins by the smallest labeled subset we evaluate in 40%, with the observed crossover at a median of ~6% of the training set. In-context few-shot examples do not behave like training - error versus shot count does not follow a power law - and the same protocol re-run by independent implementers varies with a coefficient of variation of 0.148. A controlled probe indicates the LLM depends on recognizable feature-name semantics, which plausibly makes our crossover a conservative estimate (we do not claim memorization). For a typical business table, the evidence is clear: collect a few hundred labels and train a gradient-boosted model.

</details>


### [344] [Accuracy Is Not Enough: A Cross-Architecture Audit of Demographic Bias in Deep Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20249)
*Dang Quang Minh,Nguyen Dung Son,Nguyen Huu Loi,Truong Viet Vu,Nguyen Thai Anh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对四种深度知识追踪架构（DKT、DKVMN、SAKT、AKT）在Eedi与OULAD两个数据集上、三种训练机制下进行人口统计学公平性审计，发现偏见真实但依情境而定、最准的模型最偏、标准去偏方法无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于知识追踪模型人口统计学公平性的证据几乎全部来自贝叶斯知识追踪（BKT），而驱动现代系统的深度模型缺乏可比的跨架构审计，存在明显空白。

Method: 训练4种架构×3种训练机制（标准、重加权、对抗去偏）×2个含人口统计学元数据的公开数据集（Eedi 15.9M交互、OULAD预处理后167k）；评估采用ABROCA、学生级bootstrap置信区间及置换检验，以应对近期对公平性指标不稳定的批评。

Result: (i) 偏见真实但依情境：所有架构在Eedi上社会经济ABROCA显著（0.018–0.023, p<0.005），弱势群体分组AUC更低；性别偏见在OULAD上四种架构中三种显著（多重校正后），Eedi上可忽略。(ii) 最准的最偏：AKT凭Rasch嵌入获得约4个AUC点且社会经济ABROCA最大，配对bootstrap下显著（p≤0.002）；仅消融Rasch嵌入可同时消除精度增益与超额偏见。(iii) 标准缓解不可靠：重加权与对抗去偏在保持精度的所有配置下几乎不改变ABROCA，即便对抗器在全反转强度下仅达随机水平，且弱强度阳性对照排除探针失效。

Conclusion: 深度知识追踪的人口统计学偏见依赖情境且与模型精度正相关（AKT及其Rasch嵌入），而主流去偏方法在保持精度时基本无效，亟需更可靠的公平性缓解方案。

Abstract: Deep knowledge tracing (DKT) models implicitly decide which students an adaptive system believes have mastered a skill, yet almost all evidence on their demographic fairness comes from Bayesian knowledge tracing; the deep models that power modern systems have received no comparable cross-architecture audit. We close this gap: four architectures (DKT, DKVMN, SAKT, AKT) trained under three regimes (standard, reweighting, adversarial) on two public datasets with demographic metadata, Eedi (15.9M interactions) and OULAD (167k after preprocessing), evaluated with ABROCA, student-level bootstrap confidence intervals, and permutation tests addressing recent critiques of fairness-metric instability. Three findings emerge. (i) Bias is real but context-dependent: every architecture shows a significant socioeconomic ABROCA on Eedi (0.018-0.023, $p<0.005$), with per-group AUC lower for economically disadvantaged students, while gender bias is significant on OULAD for three of four architectures after multiplicity correction yet negligible on Eedi. (ii) The most accurate architecture is the most biased: AKT gains about 4 AUC points from item-level Rasch embeddings and shows the largest socioeconomic ABROCA, exceeding every other architecture under a paired bootstrap ($p\leq0.002$); ablating only the Rasch embeddings removes the accuracy gain and the excess bias together. (iii) Standard mitigation is unreliable: reweighting and adversarial debiasing leave ABROCA essentially unchanged in every configuration that preserves accuracy, even though the adversary is pinned at chance at full reversal strength and a weak-strength positive control rules out a dead probe.

</details>


### [345] [How Far Can Sub-3B Open Language Models Go in Zero-Shot Essay Scoring on an 8 GB Consumer GPU?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20250)
*Nguyen Dung Son,Dang Quang Minh,Nguyen Huu Loi,Truong Viet Vu,Nguyen Thai Anh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在单张8GB消费级GPU上本地运行sub-3B开源模型（Qwen2.5、SmolLM2）做零样本作文评分，探索隐私受限场景下自动评分的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本作文评分多用专有API模型，但最需自动评分的公立学校因隐私规则无法将学生作文发送给第三方API，因此需评估本地小模型还能保留多少能力。

Method: 在单张8GB GPU、FP16下用四个指令微调模型（Qwen2.5 0.5B/1.5B/3B，SmolLM2 1.7B）评测ASAP-AES全部八个提示，采用自助法置信区间、Holm校正配对检验，并对关键设计选择做贴近部署的变体测试。

Result: (i) 评分细则分解提示优于整体提示，1.5-1.7B规模下两家族均值聚合差异仅0.01；(ii) 评分区间映射对校准脆弱，一个模型将特征压缩到2-4低带导致均值聚合崩溃，Multi-Trait Specialization的min-max归一化将其修复（macro QWK 0.204→0.388），冻结统计后仍在0.03内；(iii) 十二种配置中十一种的有符号误差随作文长度下降，与大LLM评委的冗长偏差相反。

Conclusion: 最佳本地配置QWK 0.388远低于人类互评上限0.769和仅长度基线0.523，sub-3B本地模型应严格定位为形成性、人工监督的反馈工具。

Abstract: Zero-shot essay scoring with large language models is usually demonstrated with proprietary API models, yet the settings where automated scoring is most needed, such as public schools grading thousands of essays under strict privacy rules, are often those where sending student writing to a third-party API is unacceptable. We ask how much capability survives when the model must be a sub-3B open model running fully locally in FP16, with a controlled study of four instruction-tuned models from two families (Qwen2.5 at 0.5B/1.5B/3B, SmolLM2 at 1.7B) on all eight ASAP-AES prompts on a single 8 GB consumer GPU, with bootstrap confidence intervals, Holm-corrected paired tests, and deployment-realistic variants of the key design choices. Three findings emerge. (i) Rubric-decomposed prompting beats holistic prompting for every model under batch min-max aggregation (though Qwen2.5-3B drops significantly on one prompt), and under mean aggregation two unrelated families land within 0.01 at the 1.5-1.7B scale. (ii) Mapping trait scores into the prompt range is fragile to grader calibration: one model compresses traits into a narrow low band (2-4 on 0-10) and naive mean aggregation collapses, while the min-max normalization of Multi-Trait Specialization repairs it (macro QWK 0.204 to 0.388) and stays within 0.03 when its statistics are frozen on 30 held-out essays. (iii) Signed error falls with essay length in eleven of twelve configurations, opposite to the verbosity bias reported for large LLM judges; normalized rubric decomposition largely flattens this slope for well-calibrated models. We anchor results honestly: the best local configuration (0.388) remains far below both the human inter-rater ceiling (0.769) and a length-only baseline (0.523), so we position sub-3B local models strictly for formative, human-supervised feedback.

</details>


### [346] [Improving Online Reinforcement Learning via Bidirectional Behavior Prior Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20268)
*Gong Gao,Xiao Lai,Jiaji Shen,Ning Jia,Xianhui Liu,Weidong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出B2PD算法，用动作价值先验引导CVAE生成高价值行为支撑集，蒸馏入智能体，实现稳定高效在线RL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线RL样本效率低、学习不稳定，源于critic估计误差被贪婪策略更新放大。现有行为先验方法依赖离线预训练，但离线数据质量有限，缺少专家轨迹，难以提供高价值策略指导在线学习。

Method: 脱离传统行为先验思路，提出双向行为先验蒸馏（B2PD）：用动作价值先验引导条件变分自编码器（CVAE）生成高价值行为支撑集，将专家行为先验蒸馏进智能体，并建立双向知识流机制。

Result: 在状态和像素任务上的实验表明，B2PD显著提升样本效率，同时保持稳定的策略优化。

Conclusion: 在线学习中引入高质量行为支撑可有效缓解critic误差放大，使结构化行为先验以有原则、高样本效率的方式引导策略更新。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms frequently exhibit poor sample efficiency and unstable learning dynamics, stemming from systematic critic estimation errors that are exacerbated by greedy policy updates. Existing behavior-prior reinforcement learning methods attempt to alleviate this issue by relying on offline pre-training to learn behavior models from fixed datasets and using policy priors to constrain online policy updates. However, the limited quality of offline datasets often hinders the ability to provide high-value policies that can effectively guide policy updates. The absence of expert trajectories significantly impairs online policy learning, leading to low sample efficiency and suboptimal performance. To address these challenges, we depart from conventional behavior prior approaches and propose a Bidirectional Behavior Prior Distillation (B2PD) algorithm. B2PD leverages action-value priors to guide a conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) in generating a high-value behavior support set. The resulting expert behavior priors are further distilled into the agent, effectively reducing inefficient exploration and enabling stable policy optimization, while establishing a bidirectional knowledge flow mechanism. Empirical evaluations on both state- and pixel-based tasks verify that B2PD substantially improves sample efficiency while maintaining stable policy optimization. More broadly, this work shows that enforcing high-quality behavioral support during online learning effectively mitigates critic-induced error amplification, enabling structured behavior priors to guide policy updates in a principled and sample-efficient manner.

</details>


### [347] [Placement Is Free, Composition Is Not: The Latin Square as a Provably-Balanced Construction for Heterogeneous Sequence-Mixer Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20269)
*Taebong Kim,Youngsik Hong,Minsik Kim,Sunyoung Choi,Jaewon Jang,Minseo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Aether-7B-5Attn模型，用7×7拉丁方排列7种序列混合机制，并通过参数匹配代理实验证明：性能主要取决于异构机制的深度分布，而非具体排列顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPT之后多数Transformer在每层重复相同注意力机制，但这更多是惯例而非经检验的结论。当多种序列混合器组合在一个栈中时，改进可能来自机制选择、放置位置或二者兼有，难以进行因果归因。

Method: 构建6.59B参数（约2.98B激活）的MoE模型Aether-7B-5Attn，49层含7种序列混合机制，按7×7拉丁方排列，保证每种机制在每行每列恰好出现一次。另建参数匹配代理模型：16层、4种机制按4×4拉丁方排列，匹配700.9M参数，每组8个随机种子训练。

Result: 重排分布式异构栈为均衡周期循环仅改变验证损失0.16%，说明精确放置影响很小；将机制聚成连续深度带惩罚0.59%；替换为同构栈惩罚1.68%。在2.16倍规模（1.514B参数）验证：同构栈惩罚增至2.63%，移除SSM家族机制导致3.20%退化。

Conclusion: 性能主要取决于跨深度分布的异构组合，而非任何特定排列。还报告了各机制成本曲线、英语与韩语评估及全部49层的因果安全审计，并发布权重、训练配方、代码与日志。

Abstract: Since GPT, most Transformers have repeated the same attention mechanism at every layer. Yet this design is largely a convention rather than a tested conclusion. When multiple sequence mixers are combined in one stack, improvements may arise from mechanism choice, placement, or both, making causal attribution difficult. We introduce Aether-7B-5Attn, a 6.59B-parameter mixture-of-experts model ($\approx$2.98B active) whose 49 layers contain seven sequence-mixing mechanisms arranged as a $7\times7$ Latin square. Because each mechanism appears exactly once in every row and column, the design guarantees balanced exposure across depth while eliminating placement confounds. To evaluate this principle, we build a parameter-matched proxy with four mechanisms arranged as a $4\times4$ Latin square over sixteen layers, matched to 700.9M parameters and trained with eight seeds per arm. The results reveal a clear dissociation. Rearranging a distributed heterogeneous stack into a balanced periodic cycle changes validation loss by only 0.16\%, indicating that exact placement has little effect. In contrast, clustering the same mechanisms into contiguous depth bands incurs a 0.59\% penalty, while replacing the heterogeneous stack with a homogeneous one incurs a 1.68\% penalty. These results indicate that performance depends primarily on heterogeneous composition distributed across depth rather than on any particular permutation. We confirm this finding at 2.16$\times$ larger scale (1.514B parameters), where the homogeneous-stack penalty increases to 2.63\% and removing the SSM-family mechanism produces a 3.20\% degradation. We further report per-mechanism cost profiles, English and Korean evaluations, and a causal-safety audit of all 49 layers. We release model weights, training recipes, training code, logs, and architecture source code.

</details>


### [348] [Labeled Incidence Structures for Native Transformer Modeling of Text, Knowledge Graphs, and Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20278)
*Mahesh Godavarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出标签化关联结构 (LIS)，用统一的 $(x_d, s, e)$ 表示文本、知识图谱和超图，使标准 transformer 无需展平即可原生处理，并通过算子编码结构差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本、知识图谱、超图都含有在关系实例中扮演不同角色的元素，但展平为 token 序列会丢失这些结构信息。作者希望找到一种统一表示，保留结构并支持单一架构处理多种数据类型。

Method: 定义 LIS，将每个端点编码为内容 $x_d$、角色/槽 $s$ 和关系实例 $e$。用槽算子和实例算子组合出结构地址 $A(s,e)=R_s R_e$，刻画其唯一且路径无关的条件；用相对传输 $P_{j\to i}=A_i^{-1}A_j$ 为注意力引入角色与关系感知的归纳偏置；并分析持久知识仓库中标识符与容量问题。

Result: 证明了加性编码（位置项 + 关系项）无法捕捉 $s$ 与 $e$ 的联合依赖；由内容计算关系实例算子可避免存储顺序敏感问题，并在固定架构和 Lipschitz 假设下得到与仓库规模 $M$ 无关的容量上界。

Conclusion: LIS 提供了一种统一、不依赖展平的结构表示，可通过算子而非架构承载结构差异，并给出了地址唯一性、相对传输注意力、加性编码局限及持久知识仓库容量方面的理论分析。

Abstract: Text, knowledge graphs, and hypergraphs all have elements that play distinct roles within relation instances, structure that is lost when data is flattened into token sequences. We introduce labeled incidence structures (LIS), a uniform representation that encodes each endpoint as $(x_d, s, e)$: content $x_d$, a role or slot $s$, and the relation instance $e$ in which that role appears. Because every data type maps to the same $(x_d, s, e)$ representation without flattening, a single standard transformer can process them all natively, structural differences are carried entirely by the operators, not the architecture.
  LIS assigns a structural address to each endpoint by composing a slot operator and an instance operator, $A(s,e) = R_s R_e$. We characterize when this factorization gives every token a unique, path-independent address. When it does, the natural operator comparing endpoint $j$ to endpoint $i$ is the relative transport $P_{j\to i} = A_i^{-1} A_j$, which gives attention a role- and relation-aware inductive bias without imposing an arbitrary sequence order.
  Additive encodings of the form "position term plus relation term" can miss information that depends jointly on $s$ and $e$. We prove this in a controlled example family: when the journey operator is approximated by the sum of a position-only term and a relation-only term, the approximation cannot capture how position and relation combine, only their separate effects.
  We also analyze persistent knowledge repositories. Identifiers tied to storage locations make models sensitive to storage order, while freely learned identifiers can become harder to control as the repository size $M$ grows relative to the sample size $n$. Computing relation-instance operators from content avoids this storage-order issue and yields a capacity bound independent of $M$, under fixed architectural and Lipschitz assumptions.

</details>


### [349] [Personalising a Cross-User Surface Electromyography Encoder Under a Small Calibration Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20296)
*Jethro Odeyemi,W. J. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了四种在固定校准预算下个性化跨用户肌电编码器的方法，发现完整微调最准，但无梯度的原型方法能以零优化器、零用户权重复制的代价恢复大部分收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人将校准视为一种"量"，却未探讨设备收集到校准重复后应如何使用它们。本文把个性化跨用户编码器看作一个有代价的设计决策，探究在相同标注数据下不同使用方式的差异。

Method: 在每个留出受试者的单一跨用户编码器上，测试四种使用相同标注重复的方法：原型自适应、线性探针、按比例微调和完整微调，并覆盖各数据库允许的最大预算范围（DB1为5次，DB2和DB5为4次重复）。

Result: 在77名受试者、各小校准预算下，完整微调在每个预算点最准，且在所有受试者子集中无例外。无梯度的原型规则无需优化器和每用户权重副本即可恢复52%–78%的收益，使设备在佩戴时可完成个性化。表征良好只需新分类器的直觉在此并不成立。

Conclusion: 各方法相对于诊所拟合的用户专属分类器的表现取决于具体数据库；工程决策上，若追求佩戴时个性化，无梯度原型方法是实用选择。

Abstract: A myoelectric interface needs calibration from the user before it will function. Earlier work has treated calibration as a quantity, but has not asked the question of what a device should do with the calibration repetitions once they have been collected. This paper views personalizing the cross-user encoder as a design decision with a cost. Four alternative approaches to using exactly the same labeled repetitions were tested from a single cross-user encoder per held-out subject. Prototypical adaptation, linear probes, scaled fine-tuning and full fine-tuning were tested at every budget up to the maximum each database allows, five repetitions on DB1 and four on DB2 and DB5. Comparing four ways to spend a small calibration budget across 77 subjects, full fine-tuning is the most accurate at every budget, consistently enough that there is no exception among subsets of subjects. The result which impacts how one might make an engineering decision however is that a gradient free prototypical rule recovers 52 to 78 per cent of its benefit with no optimiser and no per-user copy of the weights, which makes personalisation something a worn device can do at donning time. The widespread intuition that a good representation only needs a fresh classifier is incorrect here. How well each method may perform relative to a per-user classifier that would be fitted by a clinic will depend on the specific database.

</details>


### [350] [Intact-to-Amputee Transfer in Surface-EMG Gesture Decoding: Training Source and Calibration Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20297)
*Jethro Odeyemi,W. J. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究跨用户肌电(EMG)手势识别的迁移边界，将一个跨用户编码器迁移到11位经桡骨截肢者，发现零样本跨群体迁移完全失败，需少量标定数据才能超越临床基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别器通常在一个人身上训练后难以迁移到另一个人，实际使用往往需要终端用户重新标定。系统综述1077项研究显示证据薄弱：截肢者仅约占六分之一。作者希望检验跨用户编码器在截肢人群上的迁移能力及其边界。

Method: 将montage无关的跨用户编码器迁移到11位经桡骨截肢者，采用与健肢训练数据匹配的协议。对比零样本迁移、不同标定重复次数下的表现，以及不同源数据（40名健肢者 vs 10名其他截肢者 vs 二者结合）的迁移效果，并检验预注册预测。

Result: 零样本跨群体迁移完全失败；编码器需新用户标注数据才能解码。三次标定重复下，编码器比临床按用户拟合的分类器高出0.190 macro-F1（每位受试者均如此），达到0.779 vs 0.589。用40名健肢者训练比用10名截肢者迁移到新截肢者更好，二者结合效果最佳。预注册预测（截肢者EMG更难分离）仅在获得若干重复且源池加入更多截肢者后成立；单次重复及健肢匹配源的所有预算下均失败。

Conclusion: 研究界定了所提"基线强度"解释的适用边界：跨用户编码器对截肢者的零样本迁移不可行，需少量标注校准，且源池构成影响迁移效果。该结果为截肢者EMG识别提供了可操作的标定需求和迁移条件。

Abstract: A recogniser trained on one person rarely transfers to the next, and useful performance usually demands a fresh round of labelled calibration from the end user. A systematic review of 1077 studies quantifies where the evidence is thin: amputees appear in about one in six. Here a montage-agnostic cross-user encoder is carried to eleven transradial amputees on a protocol matched to its intact-limb training data. Zero-shot cross-population transfer fails outright: the encoder requires labeled data from the new user before it begins decoding, and it then exceeds the per-user classifier a clinic would fit by 0.190 macro F1 at three repetitions and for every subject in the cohort. Given three labelled repetitions it reaches 0.779 macro-F1 against 0.589 for the per-user pipeline. Training on forty intact subjects produces better transfers to a new amputee than training on ten other amputees, and combining the two produces better transfers than either individually. The prediction pre-registered for this study, which extends the encoder's baseline-strength account with the premise that amputee EMG is less separable, holds true only after a few repetitions become available and after enriching the source pool with additional amputees. At a single repetition, and at every budget under a source matched to the intact-limb comparison, it fails. Thus, it locates the boundary of the proposed account.

</details>


### [351] [A Learning Algorithm for Threshold Boolean Networks with Prescribed Fixed Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20298)
*Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种可微损失的学习算法推断具有指定不动点的阈值布尔网络(TBNs)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准方法(感知机、逻辑回归)虽能恢复不动点,但会引入大量伪吸引子,需在保持指定动力学约束下更好地推断网络。

Method: 采用自定义可微损失函数,联合强制不动点保持、惩罚伪吸引子、鼓励二值输出,并用L1正则促进稀疏性。在拟南芥FOS-GRN模型上测试,并分析稀疏系数λ的影响。

Result: 30次独立运行中5次完美重建(全部10个不动点且无伪吸引子),平均恢复8.53±0.90个不动点且无伪吸引子;标准方法可恢复至多10个不动点,但引入8至31个伪吸引子。性能在λ≤0.01范围内稳健。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效且稳定地在指定动力学约束下捕获有意义的网络动力学。

Abstract: We present a learning algorithm for inferring threshold Boolean networks (TBNs) with a prescribed set of fixed points. The proposed method employs a custom differentiable loss function that jointly enforces fixed point preservation, penalizes spurious attractors, encourages binary outputs, and promotes sparsity through L1 regularization. Applied to the FOS-GRN model of Arabidopsis thaliana, the approach achieved perfect reconstruction (i.e., all 10 desired fixed points and no spurious ones) in 5 out of 30 independent runs, recovering on average 8.53 $\pm$ 0.90 correct fixed points with no spurious attractors. In contrast, standard methods such as the Perceptron and Logistic Regression recovered up to 10 fixed points but introduced between 8 and 31 spurious ones. An additional analysis varying the sparsity coefficient ($λ$) confirmed that the method's performance and the structural properties of the inferred networks remain robust within a practical range (up to 0.01) of regularization strengths. Overall, the results demonstrate the effectiveness and stability of the proposed algorithm in capturing meaningful network dynamics under prescribed dynamical constraints.

</details>


### [352] [Improving Generalization and Robustness in Offline Reinforcement Learning via Boundary-Aware Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20300)
*Gong Gao,Weidong Zhao,Xianhui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出边界感知数据增强（BADA），利用相邻状态构建插值边界生成更符合原始分布的合成数据，提升离线强化学习的分布内泛化与鲁棒性，达 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线强化学习算法易过拟合训练数据，部署到真实环境时分布内泛化与鲁棒性差。虽借鉴计算机视觉中的正则化方法，但低层物理信号对分布偏移高度敏感，导致这些方法效果受限，难以在复杂环境中稳定表现。

Method: 理论上分析随机回合插值训练下行为策略与动作价值函数的误差界，发现误差与状态间距离正相关；据此提出 BADA，利用相邻状态构建插值边界，生成更忠实保留原始数据分布的合成数据。

Result: 在玩具环境中定性研究表明，BADA 生成的混合样本在保持策略平滑性的同时能准确重建多模态价值分布；在多个受限离线数据集与基准上取得 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: BADA 通过边界感知的插值数据增强有效缓解分布偏移问题，提升离线强化学习的泛化与鲁棒性，验证了理论分析的有效性与方法的优越性。

Abstract: Current offline reinforcement learning (ORL) algorithms tend to overfit the training dataset and exhibit poor in-distribution generalization and robustness performance when deployed to real environments, thus compromising their effectiveness. Existing methods typically enhance in-distribution generalization and robustness by leveraging regularization techniques widely used in computer vision. However, due to the high sensitivity of low-level physical signals to distributional shifts, these methods still suffer from notable limitations in in-distribution generalization and robustness, making it difficult to achieve stable performance in complex environments. To address this issue, we theoretically analyze the error bounds of the behavior policy and action-value function trained with random episode interpolation, revealing that the error scales positively correlated with the distance between states. Based on this insight, we propose a method called $\bf{B}$oundary-$\bf{A}$ware $\bf{D}$ata $\bf{A}$ugmentation (BADA), which leverages neighboring states to construct interpolation boundaries, enabling the generation of synthetic data that more faithfully preserves the original data distribution. We first conduct qualitative studies in a toy environment, showing that BADA generates mixed samples that preserve desirable policy smoothness while accurately reconstructing multimodal value distributions. Extensive experiments on limited offline datasets further demonstrate that BADA attains state-of-the-art performance across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [353] [Hypernetwork-Parameterized Spatially Adaptive Neural Operators for PDE Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20309)
*Jiaquan Zhang,Chaoning Zhang,Shuxu Chen,Meng Ye,Xiaofeng Zhang,Qiang He,Weifeng Huang,Guoqing Wang,Yang Yang,Caiyan Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出空间自适应神经算子SANO，用连续位置相关参数场替代空间共享参数，通过坐标条件超网络与局部插值机制提升异质PDE建模精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间异质PDE的动力学依赖位置，现有神经算子更新规则多为空间共享，超网络方法每实例仅生成一个全局参数化，导致共享算子难以拟合边界和高梯度区域，且误差在自回归推演中累积。

Method: SANO以傅里叶编码坐标和坐标条件超网络在采样点生成空间算子条件编码；用Hyper-Neural Element机制在局部子区域内插值这些编码，耦合相邻算子并允许其更新规则随空间变化；用单位分解权重组装重叠局部预测。

Result: 在一维、二维、三维PDE及两个穿孔域椭圆基准上，SANO持续优于竞争的神经算子、超网络和物理信息基线。

Conclusion: 空间连续的、位置相关的算子参数化能有效应对空间异质PDE，缓解局部欠拟合与自回归误差累积，实验证明其优于现有方法。

Abstract: Spatially heterogeneous partial differential equations (PDEs) exhibit location-dependent dynamics arising from variations in geometry and physical coefficients. Existing neural operators improve localized modeling through multiscale features, attention mechanisms, or domain decomposition, yet their update rules often remain spatially shared. Hypernetwork-based methods adapt parameters across PDE instances but typically generate only one global parameterization per instance. Consequently, shared operators may underfit boundaries and high-gradient regions, with these localized errors accumulating during autoregressive rollout. We propose a spatially adaptive neural operator (SANO), which replaces this spatially shared parameterization with a spatially continuous field of location-dependent operator parameters. SANO uses Fourier-encoded coordinates and a coordinate-conditioned hypernetwork to generate spatial operator-conditioning codes at sampling points. A Hyper-Neural Element (HNE) mechanism interpolates these codes within local subregions, coupling neighboring operators while allowing their update rules to vary across space, and partition-of-unity weights assemble the overlapping local predictions. Experiments on one-, two-, and three-dimensional PDEs and two perforated-domain elliptic benchmarks show that SANO consistently outperforms competitive neural-operator, hypernetwork-based, and physics-informed baselines.

</details>


### [354] [ZeroHAT: Behavior-Conditioned Zero-Shot Human Activity Trace Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20310)
*Rongchao Xu,Dahai Yu,Lin Jiang,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ZeroHAT，一个行为条件化的零样本框架，可将源区域学到的行为模式迁移并结合目标区域公开上下文，为目标区域生成合成人类活动轨迹（HAT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模HAT获取困难（采集成本高、隐私问题），合成HAT是可行替代方案。但现有方法依赖同一区域真实数据生成该区域数据，对无真实HAT的区域不可行，存在研究空白。

Method: ZeroHAT包含三个核心组件：(i) 多维一致性感知的意图提取器；(ii) 跨区域行为克隆模块；(iii) 行为条件化的活动实现模块。通过迁移源区域习得的行为模式，并用目标区域的公开上下文信息进行适配，实现零样本生成。

Result: 在十城市基准上实验：下游归一化效用达最强基线的4.5-6.4倍，平均保真度在各目标区域提升15.6%-40.8%。

Conclusion: ZeroHAT有效解决了无真实数据区域的HAT合成问题，显著优于现有基线，为跨区域零样本轨迹生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Human activity traces record individuals' timestamped visits to points of interest and are essential for applications such as mobility prediction and urban simulation. However, accessing large-scale HATs is challenging due to high collection costs and privacy concerns. Synthetic HAT generation offers a promising way to make such data available and has attracted growing interest from both industry and academia. Although many efforts have been devoted to this topic, most of them rely on real data from a region to generate synthetic data for the same region, which is infeasible for the many regions where real HATs are unavailable. To fill this gap, we propose ZeroHAT, a behavior-conditioned framework that generates synthetic HATs for a target region in a zero-shot manner by transferring behavioral patterns learned from real HATs in source regions and adapting them with publicly available contextual information about the target region. ZeroHAT has three key novel components: (i) a multidimensional consistency-aware intent extractor; (ii) a cross-region behavioral cloning module; and (iii) a behavior-conditioned activity realization module. We evaluate ZeroHAT on a ten-city benchmark, where extensive experiments show that ZeroHAT achieves 4.5-6.4x the normalized downstream utility of the strongest baseline and improves average fidelity by 15.6%-40.8% across target regions.

</details>


### [355] [EviRec: Continual Evidence Learning for Dual Cold-Start POI Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20313)
*Rongchao Xu,Lin Jiang,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EviRec，一个基于持续证据学习的POI推荐框架，通过候选级别的可靠性门控应对双冷启动问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有POI推荐方法假设用户群体与POI目录固定，但大规模数据分析（10个美国城市）显示POI流失、用户更替、类别漂移及静态记忆衰减显著，因此需要研究持续双冷启动场景。

Method: EviRec从三个视图对候选POI打分：基于用户近期移动画像的匹配视图、捕捉重复出行规律的转移记忆视图、反映候选成熟度的生命周期视图；并利用观测状态对证据进行限定，通过可靠性门控在转移记忆与生命周期证据间自适应路由。

Result: 在全年度五城市、超30,000用户、684,200条轨迹的签到数据集上，EviRec持续优于SOTA基线，冷启动查询提升最大，Dual-New场景NDCG@10相对最强基线提升20.4%。

Conclusion: 增益主要来自候选级别的可靠性门控，同时较好保留了已学习的移动规律。

Abstract: Point-of-Interest (POI) recommendation is a core task in location-based services, yet most existing methods assume a fixed user population and POI catalog. Through a large-scale data-driven analysis of 10 U.S. cities, we identify substantial POI churn, user turnover, category drift, and decay in static POI memory, motivating the study of continual dual cold-start POI recommendation. To address this setting, we propose EviRec, a continual evidence-learning framework that estimates how much historical evidence should be trusted separately for each candidate POI. EviRec scores each visible candidate from three complementary views: a matching view based on the user's recent mobility profile, a transition-memory view that captures repeated mobility routines, and a lifecycle view that reflects candidate maturity. Because a near-zero transition score may indicate either irrelevance or insufficient observation, EviRec qualifies the evidence using each candidate's observation state and applies a reliability gate to adaptively route between transition-memory and lifecycle evidence. We evaluate EviRec on a full-year, five-city POI check-in dataset containing more than 30,000 users and 684,200 trajectories. Experimental results show that EviRec consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, with the largest gains concentrated on cold-start queries. In particular, EviRec improves NDCG@10 by 20.4\% on Dual-New cases over the strongest baseline. In-depth analyses further confirm that these gains arise primarily from candidate-specific reliability gating while largely preserving previously learned mobility routines.

</details>


### [356] [Sharp Reconstruction Bounds for Autoencoders Using the Same Forward Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20333)
*Patricia Medina,Hy P. G. Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究自编码器在奇异值受约束下的重构误差下界，并验证于LiDAR森林数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自编码器前向映射对重构误差的理论极限，尤其对等奇数维、保向微分同胚情形给出紧界。

Method: 在雅可比奇异值属于[m,M]的保向微分同胚中，推导最小均匀重构导数误差；构造平移径向旋转并测试于798,452点LiDAR数据。

Result: 最小误差为max{1-M(M-m)/2,0}，仿射映射达到该界；平移径向旋转可精确重构球；LiDAR上理论界0.155约为训练误差0.185的84%，增加一隐藏坐标将误差降至6e-6以下。

Conclusion: 理论界与实验吻合，需附加条件才能得到有限数据界，增加隐藏维度可极小化重构误差。

Abstract: We study reconstruction in autoencoders that apply the same forward map before and after setting the observed coordinates to zero. For equal odd input and hidden dimensions $d\geq 3$, among orientation-preserving diffeomorphisms whose Jacobian singular values lie in $[m,M]$, we show that the least uniform reconstruction-derivative error is $\max\{1-M(M-m)/2,0\}$, with affine maps attaining this sharp bound at every prescribed depth. A translated radial rotation can nevertheless reconstruct any prescribed ball exactly with singular values arbitrarily close to one, motivating additional conditions for a finite-data bound. We test this prediction on a 798,452-point terrestrial LiDAR forest scan. At input scale $0.05$, the mean theoretical bound is $0.155$, about $84\%$ of the mean normalized training error $0.185$ across four spatial regions, two depths, and three seeds. At this scale, adding one hidden coordinate reduces the mean reconstruction error below $6\times10^{-6}$.

</details>


### [357] [COMPASS: Ordered Clustered Routing at 100K Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20352)
*Ido Greenberg,Hugo Linsenmaier,Piotr Sielski,Shie Mannor,Alex Fender,Gal Chechik,Eli Meirom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 COMPASS 算法求解有序聚类旅行商问题（OCTSP），结合搜索与学习加速的路由，通过并行子求解器协调优化，可扩展至 10 万合成节点和 2.85 万真实电商节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模路由常需按指定顺序访问节点簇，即 OCTSP。独立优化每个簇看似自然，但会忽略非局部依赖，导致次优解。

Method: COMPASS 结合搜索与学习加速的路由，协调并行子求解器；利用聚类结构，可在簇规模的指数时间内求得精确解（而非实例规模）。支持通用距离矩阵，不限于坐标输入。

Result: 实验上持续优于替代方法；扩展到 10 万合成节点与 2.85 万真实电商节点，后者为已知最大的非对称距离路由解，超出既有 ATSP 基准 9 倍。

Conclusion: COMPASS 无质量上限、随算力提升持续改进，能处理大规模非对称距离路由问题，具备良好扩展性。

Abstract: Large-scale routing often requires visiting clusters of nodes in a prescribed order, giving rise to the Ordered Clustered Traveling Salesman Problem (OCTSP). Optimizing each cluster independently seems natural, but misses non-local dependencies. We introduce the COMPASS algorithm for OCTSP, which combines search with learning-accelerated routing by orchestrating parallel sub-solvers. COMPASS has no quality ceiling and its solutions keep improving with compute. It exploits the clustered structure, and can reach exact solutions in time exponential in cluster size rather than instance size. Empirically, COMPASS consistently outperforms alternative methods. Unlike common large-scale routing solvers, COMPASS consumes general distance matrices and is not limited to coordinate inputs. We demonstrate scaling to 100K synthetic nodes and to 28.5K real e-commerce nodes. To our knowledge, the latter is the largest reported routing solution over asymmetric distances, 9x beyond established ATSP benchmarks.

</details>


### [358] [Minimax-Optimal Online Contract Design with Unrestricted Bounded Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20353)
*Rui Ai,David Simchi-Levi,Han Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究委托人只观察结果不观察行动的重复契约设计，证明当结果数为 m 时，T 轮的 minimax regret 上界为 T^{m/(m+1)}（忽略对数因子），并给出匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 委托人无法观察代理人的行动，只能依据结果支付报酬；最优响应可能使期望利润对支付向量不连续，经典学习/契约设计方法难以直接适用。作者希望在无平滑性、无单调剩余假设下，刻画重复契约设计的可学习性与最优遗憾率。

Method: 提出有效维度约简方法，将基准归一化，即使在固定平局打破规则不满足平移不变性时也适用；随后利用显示偏好得到支付差坐标下的单调响应映射；基于该映射的 Lipschitz 参数化构建学习策略，仅使用观察到的结果类别。下界构造刻画激励损失如何跨结果维度累积。

Result: 对任意固定结果数 m≥2，T 轮 minimax regret 为 Θ(T^{m/(m+1)})（至多对数因子）；上界允许任意行动空间和代理人异质性，无需平滑性和单调剩余假设；下界显示每增加一个可契约结果都会不可避免地提高学习的最坏情形代价。

Conclusion: 该工作精确刻画了多结果重复契约设计中学习的最优速率，揭示了可契约结果数对学习复杂度的定量影响，并为无平滑、异构环境下的契约学习提供了有效维度约简与策略设计框架。

Abstract: We study repeated contract design when a principal observes outcomes but not the actions that generate them. The principal may use any bounded outcome-contingent payment vector, and the agent's best response can make expected profit discontinuous in those payments. For every fixed number $m\ge2$ of outcomes, the minimax regret over $T$ rounds is of order $T^{m/(m+1)}$, up to logarithmic factors. The upper bound allows arbitrary action spaces and agent heterogeneity, without smoothness or monotone-surplus assumptions. Its key is an effective-dimension reduction that the benchmark can be normalized even when fixed tie-breaking is not shift invariant, after which revealed preference yields a monotone response map in payment-difference coordinates. A learning policy built on a Lipschitz parametrization of this map attains the rate using only observed outcome categories. The lower-bound construction accounts for how incentive losses accumulate across outcome dimensions. It shows that each additional contractible outcome creates a precise and unavoidable increase in the worst-case cost of learning.

</details>


### [359] [The Bias of Nonlinear Two-Time-scale Stochastic Approximation under Constant Step-Sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20409)
*Djamel Rassem Lamouri,Dorian Baudry,Nicolas Gast*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究步长满足 α≫β 的非线性双时间尺度随机逼近(TTSA)，在常数步长下给出了两个迭代量的均方误差与偏差上界，界为 O(α+β²/α²)，并在 β≤α^{3/2} 时证明其紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTSA 是分析强化学习、优化与随机控制中耦合迭代算法的基本工具，但非线性双时间尺度格式在常数步长下的有限时间保证仍难以获得。

Method: 在标准稳定性、正则性与 Markov 噪声假设下，分析步长 α≫β 的非线性 TTSA，对两个迭代量围绕极限均衡的均方误差和偏差给出上界，并将初始条件、快时间尺度跟踪误差、Markov 依赖与时间尺度耦合的贡献分离。

Result: 上界量级为 O(α+β²/α²)，且在 β≤α^{3/2} 时被证明是紧的；该分解澄清了 β²/α² 项的来源，并揭示与线性 TTSA 的定性差异。

Conclusion: 非线性动力学引入了线性 TTSA 情形中不存在的额外有限时间效应，表明非线性双时间尺度算法需要不同于线性分析的新理解。

Abstract: Two-timescale stochastic approximation (TTSA) is a fundamental tool for analyzing coupled iterative algorithms in reinforcement learning, optimization, and stochastic control. However, finite-time guarantees for nonlinear two-timescale schemes remain difficult to obtain, especially under constant step-sizes. In this paper, we study nonlinear TTSA with step-sizes $α\ggβ$. Under standard stability, regularity, and Markovian noise assumptions, we upper bound the mean-squared error and the bias of both iterates around their limiting equilibria. Our bounds scale as $O(α+β^2/α^2)$, which we prove to be tight when $β\leα^{3/2}$. The analysis separates the contributions of initial conditions, fast-timescale tracking error, Markovian dependence, and timescale coupling, thereby clarifying the origin of the $β^2/α^2$ term. Our results reveal qualitative differences from the linear TTSA setting previously studied, showing that nonlinear dynamics introduce additional finite-time effects that are absent in the linear case.

</details>


### [360] [SCGFM-ART: Amortized Relational Transport for Structure-Centric Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20419)
*Xiaodong He,Xincheng Wang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCGFM-ART，一种以结构为中心的图基础模型，通过摊销关系传输（ART）将异构图对齐到共享关系图集，实现统一表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图基础模型需跨严重异构的图域学习可迁移表示，但拓扑、图规模和特征语义的巨大域偏移阻碍了统一、域无关表示空间的构建。

Method: 提出结构中心的GFM框架SCGFM-ART，通过摊销关系传输（ART）将任意图对齐到共享关系图集（由有限关系地标/基定义的通用坐标系统），直接预测端到端可复用的图到基传输方案，避免运行时昂贵的Gromov-Wasserstein优化。将图分解为：全局的关系响应坐标 + 局部的节点到角色结构对应，将异质节点属性投射到规范角色空间。

Result: 在14个跨域图级和节点级分类任务上达到SOTA可迁移性，平均排名分别为2.29和1.14；拓扑扰动分析表明节点-角色传输保留了超越全局坐标的细粒度结构；在真实基准上，摊销公式实现44.2至85.1倍的冻结目标域推理加速。

Conclusion: SCGFM-ART通过关系图集与摊销关系传输有效解决跨域异质性和语义异质性，具有理论保证、优越可迁移性与高效推理。

Abstract: Graph foundation models (GFMs) aim to learn transferable representations across severely heterogeneous graph domains. However, severe domain shifts in topology, graph scale, and feature semantics impede the construction of a unified, domain-agnostic representation space. To address this, we propose SCGFM-ART, a structure-centric GFM framework that aligns arbitrary graphs onto a shared relational atlas via Amortized Relational Transport (ART). The relational atlas serves as a universal coordinate system defined by a finite set of relational landmarks (bases), while ART directly predicts reusable, end-to-end graph-to-base transport plans, bypassing costly runtime Gromov-Wasserstein optimizations. Under this formulation, SCGFM-ART decomposes a graph into a unified representation: globally via its relational response coordinates relative to the atlas, and locally via its node-to-role structural correspondences. These correspondences project disparate node attributes into a canonical role space, resolving structural and semantic heterogeneity within a singular alignment interface. Rigorously modeling graphs and atlas bases as finite measured relational spaces, we establish coordinate fidelity bounds, prove stability under predicted transport plans, and derive an amortized coverage bound that guarantees our learning objective tightly surrogates ideal relational coverage. Benchmarked across 14 cross-domain graph- and node-level classification tasks, SCGFM-ART achieves state-of-the-art transferability, securing superior average ranks of 2.29 and 1.14, respectively. Topological perturbation analyses demonstrate that node-role transport retains fine-grained structural nuances beyond global coordinates. On real-world benchmarks, the amortized formulation yields 44.2 to 85.1 times faster frozen target-domain inference by avoiding iterative alignment at test time.

</details>


### [361] [Seismic Site Response Prediction from Sparse Observations Using Finite-Element-Pretrained Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20451)
*Yi Zhu,Su Chen,Xiaojun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FLARE-T框架，通过学习并标定低维潜动力学，融合稠密有限元模拟与稀疏实测记录，改进多深度场地地震反应预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值场地反应预测常与观测不符，但实测记录受传感器覆盖和事件数量限制，难以直接修正偏差。

Method: FLARE-T从密集有限元模拟中学习低维响应流形与输入驱动动力学；训练稀疏编码器将模拟传感器响应映射到该潜坐标，并用有限记录标定潜动力学。用短响应窗口初始化预测，由完整基底运动驱动响应。

Result: 在分层土离心机试验和Lotung竖向台阵上评估：测试集多深度加速度时程和5%阻尼伪加速度反应谱优于原始有限元模型，各传感器误差均下降，Lotung两个水平分量亦改善；两套不同本构参数的Lotung震源模型精度相当。

Conclusion: FLARE-T以数据高效方式结合稠密数值响应与有限实测记录，降低对精确先验标定的依赖，可改进未来场地反应预测。

Abstract: Numerical site-response predictions often deviate from observations, yet correcting these discrepancies is difficult because records are limited in both sensor coverage and number of events. This study proposes the Transfer-Enabled Forced Latent Autoencoder for Response Equations (FLARE-T) to improve these predictions by learning and calibrating low-dimensional latent dynamics that connect the base acceleration input to acceleration outputs at multiple depths. FLARE-T learns a low-dimensional response manifold and input-driven dynamics from dense finite-element simulations. It then trains a sparse encoder to map simulated sensor responses into the learned coordinates and uses limited records to calibrate the dynamics within them. A short response window initializes each prediction, while the complete base motion drives the response. The framework was evaluated using a layered-soil centrifuge test and the Lotung field vertical array. Test-set results show that FLARE-T improved multi-depth acceleration histories and 5%-damped pseudoacceleration response spectra relative to the original finite-element models, reducing errors at every evaluated sensor for motions of different intensities and, at Lotung, for both horizontal components. Two Lotung source models with different constitutive parameters achieved comparable test-set accuracy, indicating reduced dependence on precise prior calibration. FLARE-T therefore provides a data-efficient means of combining dense numerical response information with limited field records to improve future site-response predictions.

</details>


### [362] [Training Neural Networks to Approach the Optimum Bayes Estimator in Dense Multi-Emitter Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20465)
*Yi Sun,Mona Sharifi,Muzna Yumman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在合成帧上训练神经网络以逼近密集发射体定位的贝叶斯最优估计器，结果支持未来用神经网络实现高通量大视场超时空分辨率SMLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集发射体定位中，传统方法难以逼近贝叶斯最优估计器；同时希望实现高通量、大视场的超时空分辨率单分子定位显微镜（SMLM）。

Method: 利用合成的图像帧训练神经网络，使其逼近密集发射体定位的贝叶斯最优估计器。

Result: 训练后的神经网络能够逼近该贝叶斯最优估计器，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该结果支持后续进一步研究，通过训练神经网络来实现高通量、大视场的超时空分辨率SMLM。

Abstract: We train neural networks on synthesized frames to approach the optimum Bayes estimator for dense emitter localization. The result justifies the future work on training neural networks to achieve high-throughput large-FOV super spatiotemporal resolution SMLM.

</details>


### [363] [Deep Learning-Based Classification of Cognitive and Resting States Using Electroencephalography Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20467)
*K. A. Januka S. Fernando,Harshit Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种CNN+GRU深度学习框架，结合时频分析，对EEG信号进行静息态与认知态分类，取得约76%–83%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从EEG信号中区分认知态与静息态对理解大脑活动变化很重要，而深度学习可自动提取EEG复杂数据中的重要模式。

Method: 采用时频分析提取信号显著特征；构建CNN堆叠GRU的2D-Net框架提取特征，并与传统深度学习和机器学习分类器进行对比评估。

Result: 静息-数学任务分类准确率83.177%，静息-记忆任务76.107%，静息-音乐任务83.432%，均优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 结合信号处理与深度学习方法能有效利用EEG信号区分静息态与认知态。

Abstract: The categorization of cognitive and resting states derived from electroencephalography (EEG) signals is crucial for comprehending fluctuations in brain activity linked to various mental states. EEG provides a non-intrusive approach for documenting brain function in both resting and task-oriented cognitive conditions, whilst deep learning techniques enable the automatic extraction of significant patterns from intricate EEG data. This study presents a deep learning framework to distinguish between resting and cognitive states through EEG records. The proposed framework integrates a Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) stacked with a Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU) for the extraction of features from EEG signals. Time-frequency analysis is conducted to explore the salient aspects of signals, and the derived features are then assessed utilizing conventional deep learning and machine learning classifiers, including the suggested 2D-Net architecture. The proposed approach and feature extraction strategy outperform the evaluated comparative methods, achieving accuracies of 83.177% for resting-versus-mathematical task classification, 76.107% for resting-versus-memory task classification, and 83.432% for resting-versus-music task classification. The findings illustrate the efficacy of integrating signal processing with deep learning methodologies to discriminate resting from cognitive states utilizing EEG signals.

</details>


### [364] [Don't Mask the Environment: Observation Supervision Changes How Agents Explore Under RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20715)
*Juzheng Zhang,Disha Makhija,Manoj Ghuhan Arivazhagan,Vinayshekhar Bannihatti Kumar,Rashmi Gangadharaiah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ActObs：在SFT时不仅监督动作token，还监督轨迹中已有的观测token，从而为后续RL提供更好初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SFT只对智能体生成的动作token计算损失，观测仅作上下文，未用于预测目标。作者质疑这种惯例是否为后续强化学习提供了最佳初始化。

Method: ActObs在每条轨迹中同时监督已存在的观测token，让策略学习预测动作后果，且不增加数据、参数、序列token或前向计算。

Result: SFT后两者表现相近，但GRPO后分化：Qwen3-4B在Terminal-Bench 2.0上每个采样预算pass@k更高；Qwen3-8B以部分pass@1换更高pass@k（pass@16 +3.4pp），解决更多不同任务；跨域代码编辑aider-polyglot上4B模型pass@1 +4.2pp。ActObs在RL中保留更多熵，策略移动更少，更接近SFT初始化。

Conclusion: 分析表明动作与观测梯度迅速正交，仅动作训练残留大观测梯度并损害环境预测。联合监督可防止单侧专化，保留后果预测能力，为下游探索做好准备。

Abstract: Agent trajectories record what an agent does and what happens next. Yet standard supervised fine-tuning (SFT) applies loss only to agent-authored action tokens, using environment observations as context but not as prediction targets. We ask whether this convention provides the best initialization for subsequent reinforcement learning. We introduce ActObs, which also supervises the observation tokens already present in each trajectory. Although deployed agents never generate observations, learning to predict them encourages the policy to model action consequences without adding data, parameters, sequence tokens, or forward passes. The methods perform similarly after SFT but diverge after GRPO. On Qwen3-4B, GRPO from ActObs achieves higher pass@k at every evaluated sampling budget than its action-only counterpart on Terminal-Bench 2.0. On Qwen3-8B, it trades some pass@1 reliability for higher pass@k (+3.4 pp at pass@16) and solves more distinct tasks. The advantage extends to cross-domain code editing on aider-polyglot (+4.2 pp at pass@1 at 4B), whose tasks are unseen during SFT and RL. ActObs retains more entropy during RL while requiring less policy movement, leaving the final policy closer to its SFT initialization. Our analysis traces this difference to SFT: action and observation gradients rapidly become orthogonal, while action-only training leaves a large residual observation gradient and degrades environment prediction below the base model. Joint supervision prevents this one-sided specialization, preserving consequence prediction and preparing the policy for downstream exploration.

</details>


### [365] [Distributionally Robust Federated Learning with Multi-Source Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20501)
*Yingzhu Liu,Zhongkui Li,Pengcheng You,Ashish Cherukuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对联邦学习中跨客户端混合分布不确定性与客户端内分布模糊性问题，构造局部模糊集的容许混合并集作为全局模糊集，给出可分离重构、泛化保证与联邦算法及其收敛性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法应对跨客户端混合不确定性时采用最坏情况混合优化，但假设客户端级分布估计准确；然而基于有限样本的估计往往不可靠，且真实混合比例常未知，因此需同时处理跨客户端混合不确定性与客户端内分布模糊性。

Method: 构造全局模糊集为各局部模糊集的容许混合之并集，支持客户端特定模糊半径，并得到按客户端可分离的重构形式；基于该结构建立高概率的样本外性能保证；针对惩罚型重构开发联邦算法，并在较温和正则条件下证明其收敛性。

Result: 建立了高概率样本外性能保证；证明了联邦算法在更弱正则条件下的收敛性；仿真实验验证了算法有效性。

Conclusion: 通过联合建模跨客户端混合不确定性与客户端内分布模糊性，所提框架在理论上提供泛化与收敛保证，并在实验中表现有效。

Abstract: Federated learning trains a shared model from private client data. In practice, data-generating distributions may differ, and the true mixture across clients is often unknown, making the underlying group distribution difficult to specify. Existing approaches address cross-client mixture uncertainty by optimizing against the worst-case mixture, yet assume accurate client-wise distribution estimates. However, these estimates can be unreliable when based on finite samples. To handle both cross-client mixture uncertainty and within-client distributional ambiguity, we construct a global ambiguity set as the union of admissible mixtures of local ambiguity sets. The construction allows client-specific ambiguity radii and admits a client-wise separable reformulation. Leveraging this structure, we establish a high-probability out-of-sample performance guarantee. We further develop a federated algorithm for a penalty-based reformulation and prove its convergence under milder regularity conditions. Simulations validate the algorithm's effectiveness.

</details>


### [366] [Radio Frequency Detection and Classification of Microplastics in Water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20507)
*Jaden Tolbert,Md Saiful Islam,Pingshan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种机器学习辅助的射频介电光谱流式细胞术平台，用于无标记检测和分类微/纳米塑料颗粒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微纳塑料污染日益严重，但随粒径减小，传统光学与光谱方法在通量和样品制备上面临挑战，急需快速无标记检测手段。

Method: 将8种标称直径10 μm的塑料颗粒悬浮于去离子水中，在0.2–9 GHz的4个频率下测量射频散射参数(S参数)的变化，并以此训练监督式机器学习模型进行材料分类，包括混合样品和盐水环境中的识别。

Result: 在去离子水中8类塑料的宏平均F1值、精确率和召回率均超过0.71；在含3.3%和6.6%海盐的盐水介质中，PET分类性能基本保持。

Conclusion: 结果证明机器学习辅助射频DiSC用于水环境中快速单颗粒塑料分类的可行性；未来将改进射频校准、扩大频谱覆盖、增大训练集，并用环境老化和生物污染的微塑料进行验证。

Abstract: Micro- and nano-plastic particles (MPs/NPs) are ubiquitous environmental contaminants whose increasing abundance and potential health impacts have created an urgent need for rapid, label-free detection methods. As particle size decreases to the low-micrometer range, conventional optical and spectroscopic techniques become increasingly challenging because of limited throughput and/or complex sample preparation. In this work, we present a machine learning (ML)-assisted radio-frequency (RF) dielectric spectroscopic cytometry (DiSC) platform for the label-free detection and classification of MPs. Eight types of $ 10 $ μm nominal-diameter MP particles suspended in deionized (DI) water were characterized at four frequencies spanning $ 0.2\text{-}9\text{ GHz} $. The measured alterations in RF scattering parameters (S-parameters), referenced to the carrier medium, were used to train supervised ML models for material classification, including the identification of MPs in mixed samples and saline-water environments. For eight MP classes suspended in DI water, the proposed method achieved macro-average F1-score, precision, and recall values exceeding $ 0.71 $. Furthermore, PET classification performance was largely maintained in saline carrier media containing $3.3\% $ and $ 6.6\% $ sea salt. These results demonstrate the feasibility of ML-assisted RF DiSC for rapid, single-particle MP classification in aqueous environments. Future work will focus on improving classification performance through enhanced RF calibration, increased spectral coverage, larger training datasets, and validation using environmentally aged and biologically contaminated microplastics.

</details>


### [367] [When EOS Tokens Disagree: Understanding Length Inflation in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20511)
