*Yuxiao Yang,Tianrun Yu,Shangzhe Li,Kaixiang Zhao,Xuchao Zhang,Chetan Bansal,Huaxiu Yao,Taylor W. Killian,Weitong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究on-policy distillation (OPD)中的长度膨胀问题，发现base student与post-trained teacher之间的termination-token mismatch是重要原因，并提出将功能等价的EOS tokens视为共享语义终止动作的修正方法，同时揭示训练后期还存在另一种顽固的长度膨胀现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在on-policy distillation中，学生模型的回复会变得过长，甚至耗尽生成预算，这一问题影响训练效率和输出质量。作者希望找出导致这种长度膨胀的机制性原因，尤其是termination行为方面的因素。

Method: 作者分析base student与post-trained teacher之间的termination-token mismatch，发现两者即使声明相同的stopping set，也可能把停止概率放在不同的EOS token上。为此，作者提出将功能等价的EOS tokens视为共享的语义终止动作，并系统比较仅对齐decoding stopping set与语义级终止对齐的效果。此外，作者还按K2-Horizon不同训练阶段分析OPD中终止行为的演化。实验覆盖Qwen3、Llama与Gemma三个模型家族。

Result: 三个模型家族中均存在termination-token mismatch，且仅对齐decoding stopping set不足以解决问题；将功能等价EOS tokens作为共享语义终止动作能显著缓解mismatch导致的长度膨胀。阶段性分析表明终止偏好会在训练中显著变化，并在OPD后期出现一种持续存在、无法仅靠终止对齐消除的长度膨胀。

Conclusion: termination mismatch是OPD长度动态的重要但非唯一来源。作者发布了包含所提出termination-handling修正的实现，为后续研究OPD长度控制提供基础。

Abstract: We study length inflation in on-policy distillation (OPD), where student responses can become excessively long and even exhaust the generation budget. We identify \emph{termination-token mismatch} between base students and post-trained teachers as an important source of this behavior. Across Qwen3, Llama, and Gemma, the two models can place their stopping probability on different EOS tokens, even when their declared stopping sets are identical. This mismatch can suppress the student's preferred termination action without reliably transferring the teacher-preferred alternative. We show that aligning the decoding stopping set alone is insufficient, while treating functionally equivalent EOS tokens as a shared semantic stopping action substantially mitigates mismatch-induced length inflation across all three model families. To further understand how termination behavior evolves over training, we study OPD across different K2-Horizon training stages. This stage-wise analysis shows that termination preferences can shift substantially during training, while also revealing a distinct length inflation late in the OPD run that persists beyond termination alignment. Together, these results identify termination mismatch as an important, but not exhaustive, source of OPD length dynamics. We release an implementation incorporating the proposed termination-handling corrections.

</details>


### [368] [Parallelism, critical windows, and separations among diffusion language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20539)
*Sitan Chen,Liye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次细粒度比较了三种主流扩散大语言模型（masked、uniform、Gaussian diffusion）的并行采样能力，证明了 uniform/Gaussian 扩散的采样步数可随分布的 dual total correlation 缩放，并在随机经验测度族上证明了 uniform/Gaussian 需 \(\widetilde{\Theta}(\sqrt{d})\) 步而 masked 需 \(\widetilde{\Omega}(d)\) 步，从而首次给出可证明的并行性分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLM 的核心卖点是并行生成，但不同范式（masked/uniform/Gaussian）在并行能力上的理论比较匮乏，尤其此前只有 masked diffusion 已知能达到随内在复杂度缩放的采样步数。

Method: 通过理论分析，研究三种扩散范式的采样前向传播次数，用 dual total correlation 刻画分布内在复杂度；构造特定随机经验测度族及近似 score oracle，分析并比较各范式的必要与充分前向步数，并分析 masked diffusion 中 critical window 的渐近宽度。

Result: 证明 uniform 与 Gaussian 扩散的采样前向步数可随 dual total correlation 缩放；在特定随机经验测度族上，uniform/Gaussian 需且仅需 \(\widetilde{\Theta}(\sqrt{d})\) 步，而 masked diffusion 对某些近似 score oracle 需 \(\widetilde{\Omega}(d)\) 步，首次建立三种范式的可证明并行性分离。

Conclusion: masked diffusion 更难以并行并非因其必须锁定 token 值，而是由于 masked 采样中的 critical window 渐近地比 uniform 和 Gaussian 采样更窄，颠覆了常见直觉，并为 dLLM 范式选择提供了理论依据。

Abstract: A popular selling point of diffusion large language models (dLLMs) is their capacity for parallelism: the ability to generate sequences of text far more efficiently than autoregressive models, which require one forward pass per token. Yet among the many competing paradigms for dLLMs, from masked to uniform to Gaussian diffusion, principled understanding of how these different proposals compare in parallelism remains limited. In this work, we initiate a fine-grained comparison of the capacity for parallelism among these three leading approaches and prove the following:
  - Uniform and Gaussian diffusion can sample in a number of forward passes which scales with the dual total correlation of the underlying distribution, a measure of intrinsic complexity which can be much smaller than the context length. Previously, it was only known how to achieve this using masked diffusion.
  - For a certain family of random empirical measures, we show that $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{d})$ forward passes are necessary and sufficient to sample using uniform or Gaussian diffusion, yet there exist approximate score oracles for which $\widetildeΩ(d)$ forward passes are needed for masked diffusion. This establishes the first provable separation in parallelism between the three prevailing dLLM paradigms.
  Contrary to popular intuition that masked diffusions are harder to parallelize because they must commit to token values, the latter separation instead comes from the fact that the critical windows in masked diffusion sampling are asymptotically narrower than those in uniform and Gaussian diffusion sampling.

</details>


### [369] [Mitigating Retaliatory Algorithmic Collusion in Repeated Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20548)
*Karthik Sivachandran,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将Q学习中的合谋现象与经典“简单惩罚码”（SPC）理论联系起来，提出CURB奖励塑形框架，通过惩罚策略的条件依赖（TV距离）消除合谋均衡，并在Bertrand/Cournot重复博弈及DQN中验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习智能体在重复博弈中追求自身奖励最大化时，会在无通信、无共享设计的情况下收敛到类似显式合谋的超竞争结果。现有缓解方法多绑定于特定经济场景（如双边平台、拍卖），缺乏面向一般重复博弈的通用干预设计。

Method: 作者形式化连接了先前Q学习合谋的经验观察与经典SPC理论，证明任何非平凡SPC都会在智能体策略中引入可量化的条件依赖，可通过合作与背叛历史下动作分布的总变差（TV）距离检测。据此提出CURB（Collusion Unwinding via Reward shaping and Belief injection），在Q学习中对TV距离信号施加惩罚，理论上保证将任何SPC不动点转化为平凡不动点，从而排除由惩罚威胁维持的合谋均衡。

Result: 在Bertrand和Cournot重复博弈中，CURB显著降低了Q学习智能体的合谋程度；并进一步在Bertrand博弈的深度Q网络（DQN）智能体上验证有效，表明该机制可推广至表格型Q学习之外。

Conclusion: CURB提供了一种不依赖特定经济场景的通用奖励塑形干预，通过针对SPC诱导的策略条件依赖，有效抑制重复博弈中的学习型合谋，并具备向深度强化学习扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents trained to maximize their own reward in repeated interactions can converge to supra-competitive outcomes resembling explicit collusion, without communication or shared design. Existing mitigation approaches are largely tied to specific economic settings, like two-sided platforms and auctions, leaving open how to design interventions for general repeated games. We address this gap by formalizing the connection between empirical observations from prior work on Q-learning collusion and classical theory of Simple Penal Codes (SPCs). We show any non-trivial SPC induces a quantifiable conditional dependence in agents' policies, detectable via the total variation distance between an agent's action distributions across cooperation and defection histories. Building on this connection, we propose CURB (Collusion Unwinding via Reward shaping and Belief injection), a reward-shaping framework that penalizes this Total Variation (TV) distance signal during Q-learning and is guaranteed to convert any SPC fixed point of the dynamics into a trivial one, thus precluding collusive equilibria sustained by punishment threats. Empirically, CURB substantially reduces collusion by Q-learning agents in both Bertrand and Cournot Competition Repeated Games. We further demonstrate that CURB extends to deep Q-network agents in Bertrand competition, suggesting the mechanism generalizes beyond tabular Q-learning.

</details>


### [370] [CrystalMO-TuRBO: Multi-Objective Trust-Region Bayesian Optimization for High-precision Joint Crystal Structure Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20592)
*Joseph Agada,Yishu Wang,Arpan Biswas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CrystalMO-TuRBO，一种多目标信赖域贝叶斯优化方法，用于X射线与中子联合晶体结构精修，在两阶段优化下提升收敛性、鲁棒性与参数精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最小二乘/似然优化依赖局部搜索，难以应对非凸、噪声大且参数高度相关的景观；X射线与中子数据联合精修因敏感性互补但竞争，通常靠标量化目标与人工权重导致次优解。

Method: 将X射线与中子差异建模为两个独立目标并归一化为最大化问题；采用两阶段策略：阶段1用并行信赖域贝叶斯优化对多个标量化进行全局探索，阶段2在收缩区域内局部精细优化。

Result: 在单晶Ho2Ti2O7的实验X射线与中子衍射数据上，相比经典精修方法和贝叶斯优化基线，收敛性、鲁棒性和参数精度均有提升。

Conclusion: 多目标信赖域贝叶斯优化能有效分离全局搜索与精细优化，适用于联合晶体结构精修，尤其适合多模态衍射数据场景。

Abstract: Crystal structure refinement is a fundamental inverse problem in materials characterization, where structural parameters are optimized to reproduce experimental diffraction data. Conventional approaches, such as least-squares and likelihood-based optimization, rely on local search and often struggle with non-convex, noisy, and highly correlated parameter landscapes, particularly when integrating multiple diffraction modalities. Joint refinement of X-ray and neutron data is especially challenging due to their complementary but competing sensitivities, which are typically combined through scalarized objectives requiring manual weighting and leading to suboptimal solutions. We propose CrystalMO-TuRBO, a multi-objective trust region Bayesian optimization architecture for joint crystal structure refinement. The method models X-ray and neutron discrepancies as separate objectives and transforms the problem into a normalized maximization setting. A two-phase optimization strategy is introduced: Phase 1 performs global exploration using parallel trust-region Bayesian optimization across multiple scalarizations to identify promising regions of the parameter space, while Phase 2 conducts localized refinement within a shrinking region to achieve high-precision solutions. This design explicitly separates global search from fine-grained optimization, addressing the unique accuracy requirements of refinement tasks. We evaluate the proposed method on experimentally collected X-ray and neutron diffraction data from single-crystal Ho2Ti2O7. Results demonstrate improved convergence, robustness, and parameter precision compared to classical refinement methods and Bayesian optimization baselines on refinement of a single-crystal pyrochlore material system.

</details>


### [371] [Recursive Quantum Long Short-Term Memory for Stable Short-Horizon Temperature Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20594)
*Mu-En Lee,Yen-Ku Liu,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Yun-Cheng Tsai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比递归QLSTM与标准QLSTM在每日气温预测任务上的表现，发现递归结构在收敛速度、精度和泛化方面均更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QLSTM模型的优化行为会因随机初始化和时间上下文而显著变化，需要研究更稳定的架构。

Method: 使用多伦多每日天气数据，在相同训练设置下，对比递归QLSTM与标准QLSTM在8、16、32天输入窗口、20个随机种子下的一步气温预测（最低/最高温）。

Result: 递归模型更早达到近最优测试损失，降低了MAE和RMSE，且泛化差距更小。

Conclusion: 递归量子特征变换可提升紧凑型混合量子-经典时序模型的稳定性和样本外性能。

Abstract: Quantum long short-term memory (QLSTM) models extend recurrent sequence learning with variational quantum circuits, but their optimization behavior can vary substantially across random initializations and temporal contexts. This paper evaluates a recursive QLSTM architecture against a standard QLSTM for one-step-ahead prediction of daily minimum and maximum temperature. Using daily weather observations from Toronto and identical training settings, we compare convergence, predictive accuracy, and generalization across input windows of 8, 16, and 32 days over 20 random seeds. The recursive model consistently reaches a near-optimal test loss earlier, reduces mean absolute error and root mean squared error, and exhibits a smaller generalization gap. These results indicate that recursive quantum feature transformations can improve stability and out-of-sample performance for compact hybrid quantum--classical temporal models.

</details>


### [372] [COIN-GP: Cooperative Online Learning in Networked Distributed Systems with Partial Measurements via Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20598)
*Zewen Yang,Xiaobing Dai,Zhenxiao Yin,Hang Zhao,Zhijun Li,C. C. Chan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于观测器的动态协同学习框架，结合在线分布式高斯过程回归，解决分布式传感器网络中部分可观测下的状态与部分未知动力学联合估计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式传感器网络的系统状态与部分未知动力学联合估计具有挑战性，尤其在仅有部分状态观测时，现有分布式GP方法受限。

Method: 提出基于观测器的动态协同学习框架，融合在线分布式高斯过程（GP）回归；引入新数据采集策略并给出可行数据获取的理论条件；利用GP确定性误差界推导状态估计与模型估计的误差上界。

Result: 利用GP确定性误差界给出了联合估计的误差上界；仿真实验表明该方法优于现有分布式GP方法。

Conclusion: 所提框架在测量不完整及GP模型不完善情况下仍能实现准确估计，验证了方法的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: In this paper, we tackle the problem of jointly estimating the system states and partially unknown dynamics within distributed sensor-equipped networks, particularly in scenarios where only partial state observations are available. To address this issue, we propose an observer-based dynamic cooperative learning framework incorporating online distributed Gaussian Process (GP) regression, which enables accurate estimation despite incomplete in measurements and deficient GP models. In addition, a novel data collection strategy is introduced, with theoretical conditions ensuring feasible data acquisition. Moreover, we also derive an error upper bound encompassing state estimation and model estimation, leveraging the deterministic error bounds of GPs. Empirical simulations demonstrate the superiority of our approach compared to existing distributed GP-based methods.

</details>


### [373] [Multi-center Medical Data Mining with FL-Net - A One-stop Shop for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20650)
*Simon Süwer,Julian Klemm,Elisa Acitelli,Mathieu Almeida,Lucia Altucci,Zsolt Bagyura,Michelangela Barbieri,Zsolt-Zoltán Bedő,Rosaria Benedetti,Béla Bihari,Csongor Csalóka,Lucia Dicunta,Stanislav Ehrlich,Bjoern M. Eskofier,Sándor-József Fejér,Georg Fröwis,Walter Hötzendorfer,Alexandra Kautzky-Willer,Jens Johann Georg Lohmann,Marianna Maranghi,Lorenzo Marconi,Rudolf Mayer,Wouter Leonard Megchelenbrink,Monika Moga,Adham Mottalib,Sanjeev Mehta,Madeleine Müller,Thomas Nyström,Balázs-Attila Orbán,Paul O'Toole,Giuseppe Paolisso,Paolo Parini,Matteo Pedrelli,Enrico Petrillo,Philipp Poindl,Niklas Probul,Anastasia Pustozerova,Tanja Šarčević,Lukas Weilguny,Jan Baumbach,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 FL-Net，一个满足文献五项需求的联邦临床研究框架，整合数据调和、发现、披露控制、版本化工具与容器化流程，并在真实数据集上验证端到端能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习可实现不共享患者级数据的协作训练，但多数研究停留在模拟阶段；现有 14 个 FL 框架无一完全满足文献提出的五项需求。

Method: FL-Net 将模块化数据调和、数据发现、披露控制、安全构建的版本化 FL-Net-Tools 及容器化联邦工作流执行集成到持久网络中，支持跨研究复用调和数据与工作流。

Result: 通过数据调和、MIMIC 与 US-130 的跨研究患者发现，以及支持最多 50 个并发客户端的可复现、可审计联邦工作流，验证了端到端能力。

Conclusion: FL-Net 正于 dAIbetes 与 Microb-AI-ome 欧盟项目中开发，将覆盖 9 国 10 家医院逾 80 万患者，为可互操作、可复现且隐私保护的多中心临床研究提供实用基础。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative training without sharing patient-level data, but most studies remain simulations. Based on five requirements derived from the literature, we analyzed 14 FL frameworks and found that none fully satisfied these requirements. We present FL-Net, a novel federated clinical research framework to fulfill all requirements. It integrates modular data harmonization, data discovery, disclosure control, securely built versioned FL-Net-Tools and containerized federated workflow execution into a persistent network. It enables the re-use of harmonized data and workflows across studies. FL-Net's end-to-end capabilities were evaluated through harmonization, cross-study patient discovery across MIMIC and US-130, and reproducible, audited federated workflows with up to 50 concurrent clients. FL-Net is being developed within the dAIbetes and Microb-AI-ome EU projects and will cover over 800,000 patients across 10 hospitals in 9 countries covering longitudinal and single point in time data, FL-Net provides a practical foundation for interoperable, reproducible, and privacy-preserving multicenter clinical research.

</details>


### [374] [Epidemiological Causal Graph Identification: Challenges, Identifiability and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20676)
*Sambit Mishra,Yingying Wang,Christine K. Johnson,Urbashi Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究混合数据（有序变量与指数族变量）的DAG因果方向可识别性，证明序数-指数族节点间边方向在一般参数下可分布识别，并提出打分穷举与DAGMA掩码优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现的可识别性研究主要关注连续变量和加性噪声模型，忽略了包含有序刻度、计数和连续测量的混合数据集。

Method: 节点服从有序logit模型或单参数指数族分布；理论上证明序数节点与指数族节点间边方向的分布可识别性；计算上提出基于打分的穷举搜索及基于DAGMA的掩码连续优化框架。

Result: 数值实验验证了理论，能在马尔可夫等价类中恢复经典结构方程模型下不可识别的边方向。

Conclusion: 将序数-泊松结果推广到更广泛的指数族，为混合类型数据的因果发现提供了可识别性保证和可行算法。

Abstract: Causal discovery from observational data is fundamental to statistics and machine learning, yet determining causal direction without interventions necessitates structural assumptions. Existing identifiability research primarily focuses on continuous variables under additive noise models, often neglecting mixed datasets containing ordinal scales, counts, and continuous measurements. This paper investigates causal discovery in Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs) where nodes follow either an ordinal distribution (via an ordered logit model) or a regular one-parameter exponential family distribution. We prove that the edge direction between an ordinal and an exponential family node is distributionally identifiable for generic parameter values. Our findings generalize previous Ordinal-Poisson results to the broader exponential family. Computationally, we introduce a score-based exhaustive search and a masked continuous optimization framework using DAGMA for larger graphs. Numerical results validate the theory, recovering edge orientations within a Markov equivalence class that are unidentifiable under classical structural equation models.

</details>


### [375] [RISC-V and machine learning: a survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20677)
*Shriman Keshri,Apparna Singh,Chinmaya Kumar Palo,Shreya Adya,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于RISC-V指令集在机器学习领域应用的综述，系统评估了其现状、挑战与未来方向，并提出四大研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源处理器架构与机器学习需求交汇，推动了对可定制、高效、易用硬件的需求，因此需要梳理RISC-V在ML应用中的能力与瓶颈。

Method: 基于近期研究进行分析，涵盖学术与商业实现、软件框架及实际应用；从指令集扩展、核心实现到编译器优化和部署策略全面评估生态，并构建统一分类体系。

Result: 提出了统一的RISC-V ML实现分类法，进行了性能与设计权衡的对比分析，评估了软件工具链成熟度，并识别了指令集扩展与专用加速器的新兴趋势。发现能效、专用指令开发和框架集成方面取得进展，但存在标准化、验证复杂性和生态碎片化挑战。

Conclusion: 论文提出四个研究方向：专用神经处理扩展、自适应与模块化处理器架构、安全框架、能效多域架构，为RISC-V作为下一代机器学习基础平台提供路线图。

Abstract: The intersection of open-source processor architectures and machine learning is driving the demand for customizable, efficient, and accessible hardware. This survey examines the state of the RISC-V ISA in machine learning applications, analyzing current capabilities, challenges, and future directions based on recent research. The analysis covers academic and commercial implementations, software frameworks, and real-world applications. The RISC-V machine learning ecosystem is evaluated, from instruction set extensions and core implementations to compiler optimizations and deployment strategies. Key contributions include a unified taxonomy of RISC-V ML implementations, a comparative analysis of performance and design trade-offs, an evaluation of software toolchain maturity, and the identification of emerging trends in instruction set extensions and specialized accelerators. Findings reveal progress in energy efficiency, specialized instruction development, and framework integration, while highlighting challenges in standardization, verification complexity, and ecosystem fragmentation. The analysis proposes four research directions to address current limitations: specialized neural processing extensions, adaptive and modular processor architectures, security frameworks, and energy-efficient multi-domain architectures. These directions provide a roadmap for advancing RISC-V as a foundational platform for next-generation machine learning systems.

</details>


### [376] [Video DeltaNet: A Video-Native Hybrid Attention for Livestream Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20744)
*Haocheng Xi,Yiming Xie,Hexu Zhao,Yiwen Zhang,Michael Liu,Thomas Creavin,Kurt Keutzer,Xiuyu Li,Zhaoyang Lv,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VDN结合局部Softmax注意力与双向线性记忆，用Video Delta Attention按帧更新内存，加速视频扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型去噪时反复处理长时空token序列，注意力成为主要计算瓶颈。现有线性注意力虽在LLM中常用，但直接用于视频会丢失高保真生成所需的细粒度交互。

Method: 提出Video DeltaNet（VDN）：局部Softmax注意力+双向线性记忆。线性分支VDA每帧通过联合空间token更新内存；独立输出投影与可学习门控校准两分支；分阶段教师对齐将新路径逐步引入预训练模型。在MiniMax H3上实例化，视频间交互用混合机制，涉及文本/音频仍用Softmax，配八步蒸馏与优化SGLang服务栈。

Result: 14.3秒768p视频的DiT去噪在8块NVIDIA B200上6.70秒完成，相比50步稠密H3基线（同GPU数）加速14.5倍。

Conclusion: VDN有效缓解视频扩散注意力瓶颈，在保持生成质量的同时实现显著加速。

Abstract: Video diffusion models repeatedly process long spatiotemporal token sequences during denoising, making attention a major computational bottleneck. Linear attention offers an appealing alternative and has been widely adopted in recent large language models, but directly applying it to video models often fails to preserve the fine-grained interactions required for high-quality generation. We present Video DeltaNet (VDN), which combines local Softmax attention with bidirectional linear memory for long-range video context. Its linear branch introduces Video Delta Attention (VDA), which updates memory once per frame by jointly incorporating its spatial tokens. Separate output projections and learnable gates calibrate the two branches, while a staged teacher-alignment recipe progressively introduces the new pathway into pretrained models. We instantiate VDN on MiniMax H3, applying the hybrid to video-to-video interactions while retaining Softmax for interactions involving text or audio. With eight-step distillation and an optimized SGLang serving stack, VDN-H3 completes DiT denoising for a 14.3-second, 768p video in 6.70 seconds on eight NVIDIA B200 GPUs, corresponding to a 14.5x speedup over the 50-step dense H3 baseline on the same GPU count.

</details>


### [377] [Calibrated RF-Fingerprinting Under Interference With Heterogeneous Transmission Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20765)
*Tariq Abdul-Quddoos,Xiangfang Li,Lijun Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将RF指纹识别从单发射机场景扩展到同信道干扰场景，建模为多标签分类问题，用1D CNN检测多个重叠信号，并通过校准保证误漏率上界，在真实5G测试台上验证，准确率73%–97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RF指纹研究几乎只考虑单发射机发射场景，忽视多信号在时间和频率上重叠的同信道干扰情况，限制了实际应用价值。

Method: 将问题形式化为多标签分类，采用1D卷积神经网络（CNN）；对模型进行校准，推导标签概率的置信阈值，并给出平均假阴性数量的上界保证。

Result: 使用POWDER 5G测试台真实数据（802.11a Wi-Fi、4G LTE、5G NR）验证，校准后准确率73%–97%（取决于信道条件）；不同假阴性上界下微召回约等于1减去校准假阴性，且校准对分布外干扰具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法在真实高竞争无线环境中具有潜力，可在同信道干扰下实现可靠的多发射机指纹识别并保证不遗漏频谱政策违规。

Abstract: Radio Frequency(RF)-Fingerprinting is a spectrum monitoring technique that identifies specific transmitters based on hardware impairments imprinted within the emitted signal. Although widely researched, studies almost exclusively consider scenarios where only one transmitter is emitting at a time, limiting real world applicability. In this work, we further the study of RF-Fingerprinting by considering co-channel interference, with multiple emitted signals interfering with each other, overlapping in time and frequency. Specifically, we formulate this problem as a multi-label classification problem and employ a 1D convolutional neural network (CNN). Furthermore, the models are calibrated such that the confidence thresholds for the label probabilities are derived, with guarantees on the upper bound on the average number of False Negatives, providing a degree of confidence in not missing a true spectrum policy violation. The proposed method is validated using real world data from the POWDER 5G testbed on devices transmitting 802.11a(Wi-Fi), 4G LTE, and 5G NR waveforms. The results show accuracy as high as 97% and as low as 73% after calibration depending on channel conditions. Also calibrating for various average false negatives upper bounds achieves micro recall scores of approximately (1 - calibrated false negatives) with the calibration robust to out-of-distribution interference, demonstrating the potential of the proposed method in a realistic high contention wireless environment

</details>


### [378] [PosteriorBench: From Point Estimates to Posterior Matching in Evaluating Generative Inverse Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20794)
*Jiachen Yao,Zi-Siang Hsu,Xi Deng,Aditi Gupta,Xin Ju,Sally M Benson,Gege Wen,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PosteriorBench 基准，用高保真参考后验与五指标评估生成式逆求解器在病态反问题中的分布精度，揭示现有方法存在显著分布匹配差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式逆问题评估仅关注单一合理重建的点准确度，无法检验求解器是否捕获真实后验，尤其在稀疏/含噪观测下手病态问题中易出现模式坍缩、过度自信或平均不兼容解等问题。

Method: 构建四个物理反问题任务（Darcy 流、Poisson 源、碳捕集封存、光传输材料推断）的高保真参考后验（拒绝采样、MCMC），并设计五指标评估套件：后验均值误差、后验标准差误差、MMD、切片 Wasserstein 距离、径向平均功率谱误差，覆盖稀疏感知、低分辨、非线性前向、变噪声与多峰先验。

Result: 实验显示当前求解器普遍存在明显分布匹配差距；神经算子提升分辨率鲁棒性；引导权重与生成噪声是后验方差校准的关键。

Conclusion: 从点准确度转向分布精度评估对生成式逆求解器至关重要，PosteriorBench 提供了统一的分布匹配与不确定性量化流程，揭示现有方法不足并指明改进方向。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly used to solve scientific inverse problems, but existing evaluations still focus primarily on whether a method can produce a single plausible reconstruction. This is insufficient for ill-posed problems, where multiple solutions may be consistent with the same sparse or noisy observations. In these settings, a method can achieve strong pointwise accuracy while still failing to capture the true posterior through mode collapse, overconfident uncertainty, or averaging incompatible solutions. We introduce PosteriorBench, a benchmark for evaluating the distributional accuracy of generative inverse solvers. PosteriorBench evaluates four physics-based inverse problems: Darcy flow inversion, Poisson source recovery, carbon capture and storage, and light transport material inference. For each task, we construct high-fidelity reference posteriors using computationally heavy but established procedures such as rejection sampling and Markov chain Monte Carlo, enabling direct assessment of whether solvers recover the full set of solutions rather than the single best sample. We pair these references with a five-metric posterior evaluation suite: posterior-mean error, posterior-standard-deviation error, maximum mean discrepancy, sliced Wasserstein distance, and radially averaged power-spectrum error. These metrics assess pointwise accuracy, marginal uncertainty, distributional alignment, and global frequency fidelity. The benchmark spans sparse sensing, low-resolution observations, nonlinear forward models, varying noise levels, and multimodal priors, with a unified pipeline for distribution matching and uncertainty quantification. Our experiments reveal substantial distribution-matching gaps across current solvers, while showing that neural operators improve resolution robustness, and guidance weights and generation noise are key to posterior-variance calibration.

</details>


### [379] [Score Centering Stabilizes Off-policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20807)
*Martin Marek,Max Ryabinin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究大语言模型强化学习中训练-推理不匹配(TIM)导致的不稳定性，发现根源是随训练步累积的漂移，并提出加性的"score centering"校正项来消除漂移、稳定RL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL训练大模型对训练与推理引擎间的微小差异(TIM)极其敏感，但完全消除TIM不现实，因为会大幅牺牲rollout效率。作者旨在找到TIM下RL不稳定的真正成因并加以缓解。

Method: 作者指出TIM下RL不稳定的主因是漂移——训练与推理引擎间随每步训练累积的持续偏差。为此推导出一个加性的"score centering"校正项来抵消漂移，该校正可与重要性采样(importance sampling)组合使用。

Result: 在0.6B到30B参数模型上训练时，仅用score centering即可匹配或超越量化下基于重要性采样的方法，且不匹配越严重优势越大。在staleness实验中，score centering与重要性采样的组合优于纯重要性采样基线。

Conclusion: TIM引起的RL不稳定主要源于可累积的漂移偏差；加性的score centering校正能有效消除漂移、稳定RL，并与重要性采样互补，是一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) of large language models is notoriously sensitive to small differences between training and inference engines, often referred to as the training-inference mismatch (TIM). However, completely eliminating TIM is impractical, as it would come at a major cost to rollout efficiency. In this paper, we show that the instability of RL under TIM is primarily caused by drift: a persistent bias between training and inference engines that accumulates with every training step. We derive an additive "score centering" correction term that stabilizes RL under TIM by canceling drift. When training models from 0.6B to 30B parameters, score centering alone matches or outperforms methods based on importance sampling under quantization, with the gap growing as the mismatch becomes more severe. Because the correction is additive, score centering also composes with importance sampling -- their composition outperforms pure importance-sampling baselines in our staleness experiments.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [380] [Position Paper: Neurotransmitters as a Missing Dimension in Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20083)
*Yupei Li,Manuel Milling,Berrak Sisman,Björn Schuller*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 这是一篇立场论文，主张将神经调质（神经递质）机制作为"第三学习轴"显式建模进人工神经网络，以提升自适应与持续学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工神经网络依赖统一、局部、基于梯度的参数更新，缺乏生物系统所具有的自适应灵活性与长期稳定性，现有方法多聚焦于架构扩展或正则化、参数隔离等数学微调手段。

Method: 立场论文，不提出具体算法；受哺乳动物大脑可塑性启发，作者论证神经调质应作为与神经活动、突触可塑性并列的第三学习轴，并应在 ANN 中显式建模。

Result: 无论文实验或量化结果；作者提出论点并呼吁研究方向，强调该方向有前景但探索不足。

Conclusion: 将神经调质原理融入 ANN 设计是值得关注且尚未充分探索的研究方向，应推动其在自适应与持续学习系统中的应用。

Abstract: Artificial neural networks (ANNs), as core components of modern deep learning (DL) systems, lack the adaptive flexibility and long-term stability exhibited by biological systems. This limitation largely stems from the fact that conventional ANNs rely on uniform, local, and gradient-based parameter updates, while neglecting internal learning principles that are biological mechanisms such as neurotransmitters signalling or neuroplasticity. Consequently, many existing approaches focus on architectural expansion or mathematical fine-tuning techniques such as regularisation or parameter isolation. Inspired by the superior adaptability and plasticity of mammalian brains, we posit that neuromodulation with neurotransmitters constitutes a third axis of learning, complementary to neural activity and synaptic plasticity, and should be explicitly modelled in artificial neural networks. In this positional paper, we argue that incorporating neuromodulatory principles into ANN design represents a promising and underexplored research direction, and we advocate for greater attention to this perspective in the development of adaptive and continual learning systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [381] [From Models to Systems: A Comprehensive Survey of Efficient Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19445)
*Pan Wang,Siwei Song,Hui Ji,Siqi Cao,Heng Yu,Zhijian Liu,Huanrui Yang,Yingyan Celine Lin,Beidi Chen,Mohit Bansal,Xiaoming Liu,Pengfei Zhou,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Tianlong Chen,Jingtong Hu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理高效多模态学习(EML)领域,提出首个"模型-算法-系统"三层级结构化分类体系,并探讨跨层协同设计与"效率-效用-隐私"权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型快速扩展带来计算、内存和部署瓶颈,EML成为关键研究前沿;但当前对效率在学习栈中的表现缺乏统一理解,相关认识较为碎片化。

Method: 从300余篇经典工作中提炼洞见,构建首个结构化、从模型到系统的分类法,分为模型、算法、系统三个层级,分别对应架构精简、执行优化和硬件感知编排;并进行跨层协同的方法论综合。

Result: 提出跨层协同设计对"效率-效用-隐私"权衡的作用机制;通过对MLLM的整合性案例研究,刻画领域从结构调优到全栈资源编排的演进轨迹;给出多领域应用优化蓝图。

Conclusion: 提出向"自调节智能"范式的转变,即效率应成为模型基础设计的内在涌现属性而非事后约束;并指出开放挑战与未来方向,为构建高性能、可泛化且原生高效的多模态系统提供结构化框架。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of multimodal models has surfaced formidable bottlenecks in computation, memory, and deployment, catalyzing the rise of Efficient Multimodal Learning (EML) as a pivotal research frontier. Despite intensive progress, a cohesive understanding of what, how, and where efficiency is manifested across the learning stack remains fragmented. This survey systematizes the EML landscape by introducing the first structured, model-to-system taxonomy. We distill insights from over 300 seminal works into three hierarchical levels--model, algorithm, and system--addressing architectural parsimony, execution refinement, and hardware-aware orchestration, respectively. Moving beyond a purely categorical review, we offer a methodological synthesis of the vertical synergies between these layers, elucidating how cross-layer co-design contributes to the fundamental "Efficiency-Utility-Privacy" trade-off. Through an integrative case study of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), we trace the field's evolutionary trajectory from initial structural adjustments to modern full-stack resource orchestration. Furthermore, we provide a holistic discussion and application-specific optimization blueprints for diverse domains and posit a paradigm shift toward self-regulating intelligence, where efficiency is an intrinsic, emergent property of the model's fundamental design rather than a post-hoc constraint. Finally, we present open challenges and future directions that will define the trajectory of EML research. This survey establishes a structured framework for multimodal systems that are not only high-performing and generalizable but natively efficient and ready for ubiquitous deployment. A continuously updated version is available at https://github.com/pwang322/Efficient-Multimodal-Learning-Survey.

</details>


### [382] [Trigger Timing, Deadline Readiness, and Event-Aligned Accounting for Dynamic Ad Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19899)
*Prashant Chaudhary,Kapil Khandelwal*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出事件账本、候选不变的参考截止时间和pod级贡献核算框架，用以分离动态广告插入中的触发、到达、就绪、播放、可计费和测量事件。通过合成实验发现，播放头摘要会误分类到达事件，但未反转贡献对比；而用"截止前完成"替代"截止时有效"会在9个信息条件中的3个反转对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态广告插入的比较常将触发、到达、就绪、播放、可计费和测量等多个事件混为一谈，即便算术核算正确也可能导致误判。作者希望厘清这些事件的边界，避免因事件定义混淆而产生错误结论。

Method: 采用观察事件账本（observed-event ledger）、候选不变的参考截止时间（candidate-invariant reference deadline）和pod级贡献核算。截止规则在候选分配前固定，检验的是可接受的过渡状态是否仍有效，而非准备工作是否更早完成。提出受限单调播放表示，并给出反例。进行离线合成研究：9个短生命周期条件（信息性）和9个长生命周期条件（分析对照），共45,000个共享脚本。

Result: 播放头摘要在非单调混合中严重误分类到达事件，但在本实验网格中两种捷径均未反转贡献对比（部分误差在共享设计下抵消）。将截止时有效性替换为截止前完成，会在9个信息条件中的3个反转对比，且全部出现在某一延迟设置下。用实际观众到达计分替代参考截止会计分，会改变暂停路径就绪性但不改变贡献符号。保守边界在记录缺失时保留不确定性。

Conclusion: 这些结果是事件定义应用于已知误分类机制的必然产物。证据为合成数据，未使用商业遥测，也不对服务端插入或服务引导插入进行排名。研究强调事件定义和核算规则的选择会显著影响动态广告插入比较的结论。

Abstract: Dynamic ad insertion comparisons can conflate trigger, reach, readiness, playback, billability and measurement even when the accounting is arithmetically correct. We separate these events with an observed-event ledger, a candidate-invariant reference deadline and pod-level contribution accounting. The deadline rule is fixed before candidate assignment and tests whether an admissible transition state remains valid, not whether preparation merely finished earlier. A restricted monotone-playback representation states when media-position summaries suffice; a counterexample shows why they fail over a wider path class. An offline synthetic study exercises the definitions over nine short-lifetime conditions informative for the readiness comparison and nine long-lifetime conditions serving as analytic controls. Across 45,000 shared scripts, two trigger policies share imposed playback paths, latency draws and hypothetical value and cost coefficients. Playhead summaries substantially misclassify reach events in the nonmonotone mixtures, yet neither shortcut reverses the contribution contrast in this grid, because some errors cancel under the shared design. Replacing validity at the deadline with completion by the deadline reverses the comparison in three of the nine informative conditions, all at one of the three latency settings. Scoring readiness at actual viewer arrival rather than at the reference deadline shifts pause-path readiness but changes no contribution sign. These outcomes are consequences of the event definitions applied to established misclassification mechanisms. Conservative bounds retain uncertainty when records are missing, and the artifact records code, seeds, event histories and checking procedures. The evidence is synthetic, uses no commercial telemetry, and ranks neither server-side nor server-guided insertion.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [383] [Algebraic Retrieval: Composable Search for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19482)
*Damian Delmas*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出"代数检索"(Algebraic Retrieval)，允许AI智能体在查询时组合搜索策略，将相关性、约束与排序偏好表达为数学查询。基于PEM，作者在Vaswani数据集上验证了不同实现(demo、SQL、PyTerrier)的执行一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索系统缺乏让智能体在查询时灵活组合搜索策略、动态表达相关性标准与排序偏好的能力。

Method: 基于Programmatic Embedding Modulation (PEM)，暴露检索中的向量与分数运算，将对比评分、候选池重排、加权排序等表达为可组合查询，并提供可执行SQL与PyTerrier对应实现。

Result: 在公开Vaswani数据集(11,429篇文档)上，各程序实现选出相同文档集，分数差异低于1e-6；仅有一对并列文档在不同评分路径下排序不同。

Conclusion: 评估关注执行一致性(execution parity)而非智能体行为或检索质量，验证了代数检索多实现间的等价性。原文未给出更明确结论。

Abstract: Algebraic Retrieval lets AI agents compose search strategies at query time. Relevance criteria, eligibility constraints, and ranking preferences can be expressed together in a mathematical query. The query surface exposes available operations, so an agent can combine them for the question at hand and revise a program after inspecting results. We evaluate execution parity, not agent behavior or retrieval quality. Building on Programmatic Embedding Modulation (PEM), which exposes vector and score arithmetic during retrieval, we demonstrate contrastive scoring, candidate-pool reranking, and weighted ranking as composable queries, alongside executable SQL and PyTerrier counterparts. On the public 11,429-document Vaswani fixture, each program's implementations select the same document set with score differences below 1e-6; one tied pair orders differently across scoring paths.

</details>


### [384] [Beyond Similarity through Zero-Token Geometric Graphs for Multi-Hop RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19622)
*Zeliang Li,Xiaofen Xing,Kailing Guo,Xiangmin Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: G$^3$RAG 是一个无需 LLM 的文档级图 RAG 框架，通过几何增益评分与拓扑惩罚构建图，在多跳问答中取得最优 F1 与命中率，并消除图构建的 token 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳 RAG 需要既相关又有新颖性的证据来弥合语义鸿沟。稠密检索偏向语义相似文档，而图方法多依赖昂贵的 LLM 实体抽取且易受噪声连接传播影响。

Method: 提出 Geometric Gain Graph RAG（G$^3$RAG），仅用文档、离线构图不调用 LLM、不生成 token。为每条边赋几何增益分数 $\cos\theta\cdot\sin\theta$，兼顾方向一致性与正交性；用密度感知拓扑惩罚抑制高连接枢纽；从过滤后的查询种子出发进行单步受控扩散以获取互补证据。

Result: 在 MusiQue、2WikiMultiHopQA、HotpotQA 上，使用 Nv-embed-v2 与 Qwen3-8B-embed 两种嵌入设置，平均 F1 与答案文档命中率均优于所评估的图基线；平均 F1 最高提升 4.26 点，MusiQue 上提升 5.76 点；同时消除了实体图方法的图构建 token 成本。

Conclusion: 几何结构可在无 LLM 构图的情况下高效支持多跳证据发现。代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) requires evidence that remains relevant to a query while introducing enough novelty to bridge semantic gaps. Dense retrieval tends to concentrate on semantically similar documents, whereas graph-based alternatives often depend on costly Large Language Model (LLM) entity extraction and may propagate through noisy connections. We introduce Geometric Gain Graph RAG (G$^3$RAG), a document-only framework whose offline graph construction uses no LLM calls or generated tokens. G$^3$RAG assigns each edge a geometric gain score, $\cosθ\cdot \sinθ$, that jointly captures directional consistency and orthogonality between document representations. A density-aware topological penalty suppresses highly connected hubs, while single-step controlled diffusion expands from filtered query seeds toward complementary evidence. We evaluate G$^3$RAG on MusiQue, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and HotpotQA using Nv-embed-v2 and Qwen3-8B-embed. G$^3$RAG obtains the best average F1 and answer-document hit rate among the evaluated graph-based baselines in both embedding settings, with gains of up to 4.26 F1 points in average performance and 5.76 points on MusiQue. It also removes the graph-construction token cost incurred by entity-based graph methods. These results show that geometric structure can support efficient multi-hop evidence discovery without LLM-based graph construction. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/G3RAG-99D9/

</details>


### [385] [Self-Evolving Search Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19656)
*Sangam Lee,Wonjae Lee,Sunghwan Kim,Deogyong Kim,Jaehoon Kim,Daye Nam,SeongKu Kang,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 SELF-INDEX 框架，让检索索引无需人工干预即可自我演化，通过诊断短板的 Optimizer 与主动探索需求的 Query Simulator，在多种语料和检索器上稳定提升检索性能及下游智能体表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索质量高度依赖索引键对文档知识的表达效果，但有效表示随检索环境变化，固定优化策略难以通用；而现有索引演化仍主要依赖人工诊断失败、改进策略并重处理索引。

Method: SELF-INDEX 框架含两个核心组件：Optimizer 自动诊断检索短板，选择性修改相关索引键，并在更新前验证每次修改；Query Simulator 主动模拟额外查询需求，使索引演化超越已有可用查询。

Result: 在多种语料库和检索器上，SELF-INDEX 一致提升检索性能，并优于现有索引优化方法；收益还延伸至下游应用，提升搜索智能体的效果与效率，并帮助智能体记忆系统检索有用的历史交互。

Conclusion: 索引可以在无人工干预下自我演化并适应检索环境，主动探索需求进一步拓展优化空间，且对搜索智能体和智能体记忆等下游任务具有实际价值。

Abstract: Information retrieval is increasingly important as LLM agents tackle complex tasks involving diverse information needs. Because retrieval relies on an index that represents each document through index keys, retrieval quality depends heavily on how effectively these keys expose the knowledge contained in each document. However, effective index representations vary across retrieval environments, making it difficult for any fixed optimization strategy to perform consistently. Yet evolving an index to its retrieval environment remains largely human-driven, requiring humans to diagnose retrieval failures, refine the optimization strategy, and reprocess the index accordingly. We propose SELF-INDEX, a framework that enables an index to self-evolve without human intervention. Its Optimizer autonomously diagnoses retrieval shortfalls, selectively revises the responsible index keys, and validates each revision before updating the index. Beyond reacting to observed retrieval demands, SELF-INDEX proactively explores additional demands through a Query Simulator, allowing the index to evolve beyond the queries already available for optimization. Across diverse corpora and retrievers, SELF-INDEX consistently improves retrieval performance while outperforming existing index optimization methods. We further show that these benefits extend to downstream applications, improving the effectiveness and efficiency of search agents and helping agent memory systems retrieve useful past interactions.

</details>


### [386] [Dense Feature Representation over Sequence Modeling: A Solution to the KDD Cup 2026 UniRec Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19787)
*Yi Zhang,Weiliang Ji*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: KDD Cup 2026腾讯UniRec CVR预测第10名方案：从官方基线出发，15步单变量链将测试AUC从0.813237提升至0.827816，最终提交0.828535。消融显示增益主要来自稠密特征表示和优化器，而非序列建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在3482万条工业CVR数据上，哪些机制真正提升留出集AUC，并揭示验证集评估的泛化陷阱。

Method: 基于官方PCVRHyFormer基线，采用15步单变量改进链，并进行全模型留一消融分析；同时考察行组划分与时间窗口带来的分布偏移问题。

Result: 测试AUC从0.813237升至0.827816，最终0.828535。移除稠密特征表示栈损失0.0095 AUC，移除正交化优化器损失0.0028；而各序列建模组件（合并单流骨干、极性通道、辅助头、逐token FFN）影响均不超过0.0005，处于±0.0004种子波动带内。

Conclusion: 在此规模下，稠密表示与优化而非更精细的序列建模才是CVR AUC的主要驱动力；由于行组划分共享时间窗口导致验证AUC高估约0.014，最终结论必须以留出排行榜为准。

Abstract: We describe our 10th-place solution to the KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UniRec Challenge, industrial click-to-conversion (CVR) prediction over 34.82M records, and we ask which mechanisms actually move held-out AUC. Starting from the official PCVRHyFormer baseline, a 15-step single-variable chain raises test AUC from 0.813237 to 0.827816, and our final submission reaches 0.828535. A leave-one-out ablation from the full model attributes the gain: removing the dense-feature representation stack costs 0.0095 AUC and removing the orthogonalized optimizer costs 0.0028, while no sequence-modeling component (merged single-stream backbone, polarity channel, auxiliary head, per-token FFN) costs more than 0.0005, within or adjacent to a $\pm$0.0004 seed band. We also report a generalization hazard: the row-group train/validation split shares one time window, so validation AUC overstates the leaderboard by about 0.014; anti-memorization and high-cardinality-ID changes even invert sign against it, a divergence that traces to dump-to-dump distribution shift and survives a time-ordered re-split. Dense representation and optimization, not finer sequence modeling, drive CVR AUC at this scale, and verdicts must come from the held-out leaderboard.

</details>


### [387] [Reproducing Transparent and Scrutable Recommendations: Exploring Open-Weight Models via Natural-Language User Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19831)
*Noah Mamié,Laurin van den Bergh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 复现研究验证了自然语言用户画像推荐系统(UPR)的竞争力与透明度优势,并扩展了消融、多随机种子稳定性及机制可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原作者声称基于生成式自然语言用户画像可提升推荐透明度与可干预性,但结果的可复现性与内部机制尚待验证。

Method: 复现核心实验,并进行上下文消融实验、五个随机种子的多种子稳定性测试,以及基于nnsight框架的机制可解释性分析与反事实画像扰动。

Result: 成功复现原文核心结论:UPR在测试集重排序协议下具竞争力且更透明;但扰动画像仅均匀改变预测评分,无流派选择性效应,排序不变;问题源于评分回归目标而非画像接口。

Conclusion: UPR的透明度主张成立,但其画像干预对排序无实质影响,排序目标模型在此任务中表现明显更优。

Abstract: In this reproducibility study, we investigate the transparency and scrutability of recommender systems enhanced by incorporating generated natural-language user profiles that represent user preferences. The original paper explores the synthesis of user profiles from raw user-generated review text across domains such as movies and accommodations (Amazon Movies & TV, TripAdvisor). Crucially, these natural-language user profiles enable direct user interaction and intervention, allowing users to customize recommendations by correcting misattributed preferences or addressing cold-start settings. We successfully reproduce the core findings of the original study. Additionally, we extend the evaluation by conducting systematic context ablation experiments, multi-seed stability across five distinct random seeds to establish statistical reliability, and a mechanistic interpretability analysis using the nnsight framework to probe internal model representations under counterfactual profile perturbations. Our findings verify the original paper's claim that User Profile Recommendation (UPR) achieves competitive performance under its test-set reranking protocol and makes recommendations more transparent. Perturbing the natural-language profiles does change predictions, but it shifts predicted ratings uniformly across genres with no detectable genre-selective effect, leaving rankings unchanged even under direct activation steering. We trace this back to the rating-regression objective rather than the profile interface, with ranking-objective models clearly exceeding in this task.

</details>


### [388] [The Missing Complement: State-Conditioned Minimal Sufficient Evidence for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20050)
*Zhexi Feng,Ruiyi Zhang,Yongbo Yang,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出“状态条件下的最小充分证据恢复”任务，并构建SERBench与MSS-Complement方法，以集合级充分性而非逐段相关性检索证据，显著优于重排序基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理执行任务中途已读过大量高分段落，但相关性按段落打分，充分性却属于集合：排序器可能用同一事实的多种变体填满预算，导致决策仍缺乏支持。

Method: 形式化“状态条件下的最小充分证据恢复”：给定代理当前状态，恢复其下一步决策仍缺失的紧凑证据组合。MSS-Complement将证据获取视为集合构建：用三次语义调用提出联合充分集、搜索缺失内容，并在6,144 token内返回4-8个完整源单元。

Result: 在45个仓库的500个留出状态上，五条证据时完整恢复率73.0%，八条时80.6%；Qwen3 embedding加重排序为61.4%和72.4%，纯相似度排序为66.6%。冻结源码无金标池时领先5.0点。AMA-Bench上答桉提示缩小76.2%，准确率高出2.08点。

Conclusion: 面向代理的检索应被表述为“恢复决策缺失的内容”，而非“重排问题相似的内容”。

Abstract: A coding agent halfway through an issue has already read much of what a retriever ranks highest. Relevance is scored per passage, but sufficiency belongs to the set: a ranker can fill its budget with variants of one required fact and leave the decision unsupported. We formulate state-conditioned minimal sufficient evidence recovery: given a captured agent state, recover a compact evidence combination that supplies the support its next decision still lacks. SERBench measures this on 500 held-out states from 45 repositories, recording what the agent has seen and crediting only sets that cover every fact the current decision was annotated to require. MSS-Complement treats acquisition as set construction, not ranking. Three semantic calls propose a jointly sufficient set, search for what it lacks, and return 4-8 intact source units within 6,144 tokens. One configuration, fixed on calibration data, recovers a complete set for 73.0% of those states at five items and 80.6% at eight, against 61.4% and 72.4% for Qwen3 embedding with reranking. A matched control ranking by similarity alone reaches 66.6%, placing the gain in the set-level policy, not the computation. From frozen repository source with no gold-derived pool, the lead is 5.0 points. On AMA-Bench it answers from a 76.2% smaller answer prompt, with accuracy 2.08 points above that benchmark's own memory agent. Removing one required group from an otherwise complete set costs 12.3 and 11.1 points of repair-localization precision under two executors. Retrieval for agents is better posed as recovering what a decision lacks than re-ranking what an issue resembles.

</details>


### [389] [Think Thrice Before Reranking: Multi-perspective Evidence and Reasoning Integration for Text Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20131)
*Lijun Liu,Zhengzong Chen,Wenyan Li,Yuanyuan Zhao,Fei Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MERIT-Rank框架，通过多视角推理轨迹集成提升文本重排序的鲁棒性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM推理的重排序方法依赖单一推理轨迹，易受推理错误影响，且难以建模文档相关性的多方面信号。

Method: 构建多轨迹推理空间（MTRS）从多视角评估查询-文档相关性；设计联合重排序器整合多条推理路径为统一排名决策；提出渐进式排名策略优化（PRPO）分阶段训练，稳定推理轨迹并持续提升排名质量。

Result: 在推理密集型及传统检索基准上均优于竞争基线；4B模型在BRIGHT上超越多数7B乃至32B重排序器。

Conclusion: 多视角推理轨迹集成与渐进式优化能有效提升重排序的鲁棒性和效果。

Abstract: Reasoning-based reranking with Large Language Models (LLMs) has shown promising improvements in text ranking. However, current methods predominantly rely on a single reasoning trajectory, resulting in rankings that are susceptible to reasoning errors and inherently constrained in modeling the multifaceted signals underlying document relevance. To resolve this dilemma, we propose MERIT-Rank(Multi-perspective Evidence and Reasoning Integration for Text Reranking), a framework that models complementary reasoning trajectories to improve reranking robustness. MERIT-Rank formulates a Multi-Trajectory Reasoning Space (MTRS) that evaluates query-document relevance from multiple perspectives and introduces a joint reranker that consolidates these reasoning paths into a unified ranking decision. We further develop Progressive Rank Policy Optimization (PRPO), a progressive training framework that stabilizes reasoning trajectories while continually improving ranking quality through staged optimization objectives. Experiments on both reasoning-intensive and traditional retrieval benchmarks show that MERIT-Rank consistently achieves superior performance over competitive baselines. The 4B model notably outperforms most 7B and even 32B rerankers on BRIGHT.

</details>


### [390] [FacetCRS: Multi-Faceted Preference Learning for Pricking Filter Bubbles in Conversational Recommender System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20175)
*Yongsen Zheng,Ziliang Chen,Jinghui Qin,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出FacetCRS范式，通过多面向偏好学习与会话交互打破推荐系统过滤气泡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过滤气泡使用户接触内容狭窄、强化既有偏好。现有研究多在静态或准静态场景中考察该问题，但真实在线推荐中用户-系统反馈回路会随时间持续加剧气泡。

Method: 提出FacetCRS端到端会话推荐框架，借助自然语言会话中的及时用户-物品交互刺破气泡。将用户偏好自动建模为多面向：实体、词、上下文、评论，以捕捉多样且动态的偏好，并自适应学习各层级偏好面向表示与多类外部知识。

Result: 在两个公开基准数据集上，方法在缓解过滤气泡与提升会话推荐质量方面均达到当前最优（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: 多面向偏好学习有助于在会话推荐中打破过滤气泡并提升推荐效果。

Abstract: The filter bubble is a notorious issue in Recommender Systems (RSs), which describes the phenomenon whereby users are exposed to a limited and narrow range of information or content that reinforces their existing dominant preferences and beliefs. This results in a lack of exposure to diverse and varied content. Many existing works have predominantly examined filter bubbles in static or relatively-static recommendation settings. However, filter bubbles will be continuously intensified over time due to the feedback loop between the user and the system in the real-world online recommendation. To address these issues, we propose a novel paradigm, Multi-Facet Preference Learning for Pricking Filter Bubbles in Conversational Recommender System (FacetCRS), which aims to burst filter bubbles in the conversational recommender system (CRS) through timely user-item interactions via natural language conversations. By considering diverse user preferences and intentions, FacetCRS automatically model user preference into multi-facets, including entity-, word-, context-, and review-facet, to capture diverse and dynamic user preferences to prick filter bubbles in the CRS. It is an end-to-end CRS framework to adaptively learn representations of various levels of preference facet and diverse types of external knowledge. Extensive experiments on two publicly available benchmark datasets demonstrate that our proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance in mitigating filter bubbles and enhancing recommendation quality in CRS.

</details>


### [391] [Reasoning Quality Matters: Combating Reasoning Collapse in LLM-based Embedding Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20563)
*Zihan Gong,Xiaohan Ye,Jiangchao Yao,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CoFree两阶段框架，通过参考引导SFT和双奖励强化学习，将LLM推理融入嵌入优化，避免"推理坍缩"，在22个数据集上平均提升2.8 nDCG@10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM嵌入方法存在"推理坍缩"问题：被动特征提取或指令跟随优化会抑制推理生成，或产生与检索无关的文本，损害嵌入质量。

Method: 两阶段框架CoFree：第一阶段用参考引导的监督微调恢复基础嵌入模型的推理能力并保留表示强度；第二阶段引入嵌入导向和推理导向的双奖励，在强化学习中实现细粒度推理优化。

Result: 在MTEB和BRIGHT的22个数据集上，CoFree-4B较Qwen3-Embedding-4B平均绝对提升2.8 nDCG@10；真实检索系统在线实验也显示一致增益。

Conclusion: CoFree有效缓解推理坍缩，将嵌入学习转变为高质量推理引导的搜索过程，显著提升检索性能，代码与模型将公开。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown strong potential for producing context-rich text embeddings for retrieval. Most existing methods either treat embedding learning as passive feature extraction or exploit LLM reasoning through instruction following for better embedding optimization. However, specialization toward embedding objectives can suppress useful reasoning generation or produce retrieval-irrelevant text. We refer to these two forms of degradation as reasoning collapse. To address this issue, we propose CoFree (Collapse-Free Reasoning Embedding), a two-stage framework that progressively integrates LLM reasoning into query and document embedding optimization while preserving reasoning quality. At the first stage, CoFree applies reference-guided supervised fine-tuning to restore the reasoning ability and retain representational strength of the foundation embedding model. At the second stage, we introduce dual rewards, an embedding-oriented reward and a reasoning-oriented reward, to guarantee fine-grained reasoning of the relevance toward the embedding goal in reinforcement learning. This endpoint-coupled optimization transforms embedding learning from static alignment into a high-quality reasoning-guided search process for retrieval. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of CoFree, with CoFree-4B achieving an average absolute improvement of 2.8 nDCG@10 points over Qwen3-Embedding-4B across 22 datasets from MTEB and BRIGHT. Online experiments in a real-world retrieval system further show consistent gains. Code, RTED, and model checkpoints will be made publicly available.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [392] [Code-as-Auditor: Executable Compliance Reasoning via Regulation-to-Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19199)
*Jisoo Kim,Taeyoon Kwack,Jinwoo Jang,Woo Kyung Kim,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 Code-as-Auditor 框架，将法规转化为可执行代码结构并结合动态问答推理，实现有据可依的合规评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 用于合规与法律推理时，输出常缺乏对法律逻辑与证据的显式依据。

Method: 将法规翻译为形式化清单和可执行决策树（编码为可解释代码结构）；推理时将每个清单项动态展开为事实性与反事实性问题，引导模型基于个案证据推理；形成从证据识别到规则适用再到决策的推理流程，并用自验证循环提升代码逻辑一致性与结果可追溯性。

Result: 在隐私与数据保护场景实验中，Code-as-Auditor 给出更准确、更具证据支撑的评估。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了基于显式监管标准的自动化合规检查。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly adopted for compliance and legal reasoning tasks, yet their outputs often lack explicit grounding in legal logic and evidence. We present Code-as-Auditor, an LLM-based framework that extends the model's reasoning capability toward structured and evidence-grounded compliance assessment. The framework translates regulatory information into (1) formalized checklists and executable decision trees, encoding regulations and conditions as interpretable code structures. During inference, each checklist item is (2) dynamically expanded into factual and counterfactual questions, guiding the model to reason over case-specific evidence and potential violations. This process establishes a reasoning pipeline that proceeds from evidence identification, through rule application, to final decision-making, while a self-verification loop improves the logical consistency of the generated code and the traceability of outcomes. Experiments on privacy and data protection scenarios demonstrate that Code-as-Auditor delivers more accurate and evidence-backed evaluations, enabling automated compliance regulation checking grounded in explicit regulatory criteria.

</details>


### [393] [DeltaSelect: Affordable A/B Testing for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19607)
*Nicholas J. Conn*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 DeltaSelect，一种开源方法，用少量任务在开发中做候选与基线的反复对比，而非模型排名。案例中采用版本成本降低 58.1%（p=0.008），校准分数更高（42.36% vs 36.46%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码 agent 基准面向全面比较而非频繁开发决策：单次运行波动大、完整评测昂贵、官方评测框架可能与实际使用的不同。DeepSWE 已发布试验的重采样分析显示，仅 19.5%（113 中 22 个）任务与全基准成绩的第五百分位 Pearson 相关达到 0.50 以上。

Method: DeltaSelect：用 Pearson 相关筛选"单次运行结果能稳定跟随全基准表现"的任务；用线性回归把分数的验证器结果映射到统一得分；在美元预算约束下选出固定任务集。

Result: 在 gpt-5.6-luna 低推理案例中用于改进自定义技能与指令。13 次评测记录成本 27.86 美元（2026-08-16 公布费率）。采用版本比初始版本便宜 58.1%（1.75 vs 4.18 美元，p=0.008），校准分数更高（42.36% vs 36.46%，公布类比方差 p=0.326）。

Conclusion: DeltaSelect 面向开发期的重复基线—候选对比，不用于模型排名；开源可用。

Abstract: Coding-agent benchmarks are built for broad and comprehensive comparisons, not frequent development decisions. Individual runs vary, full suites are expensive, and the benchmark harness may differ from the harness used in practice. In a resampling analysis of DeepSWE's published trials, only 19.5% of tasks (22 of 113) had a fifth-percentile Pearson correlation of at least 0.50 with full-benchmark performance. The paper presents DeltaSelect, an open-source method that identifies tasks whose one-run results consistently track full-benchmark performance using Pearson correlation, maps fractional verifier results to a common score using linear regression, and selects a fixed task set within a dollar budget. DeltaSelect is intended for repeated baseline-versus-candidate comparisons during development, not model rankings. In a gpt-5.6-luna low-reasoning case study, DeltaSelect was used to revise custom skills and instructions. Across 13 evaluations, the recorded cost was USD 27.86 at rates published August 16, 2026. The adopted version cost 58.1% less than the initial version (USD 1.75 versus USD 4.18; p=0.008), while the calibrated score was higher (42.36% versus 36.46%; published-analog variance p=0.326).

</details>


### [394] [BA-TRACE: Boundary-Aware Trace Reconstruction for Scenario-Based Evaluation of Mixed AUTOSAR Adaptive and ROS 2 Vehicular Embedded Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19699)
*Shunsuke Ito,Ryudai Iwakami,Hiroyuki Hanyu,Tasuku Ishigooka,Takuya Azumi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BA-TRACE：面向AUTOSAR AP与ROS 2混合车载系统的边界感知追踪重构框架，联合ROS 2事件、ara::log、ARXML依赖与桥接层插桩，跨DDS-SOME/IP重建端到端执行图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合栈的执行路径跨越DDS-SOME/IP中间件边界，现有仿真器与追踪工具只能执行场景或采集平台本地轨迹，无法重建跨域数据流，导致基于场景的评估难以解释。

Method: BA-TRACE组合四类信息：ROS 2 trace事件、AUTOSAR ara::log事件、ARXML导出的结构依赖关系，以及桥接层插桩，据此重构跨越DDS-SOME/IP边界的端到端执行图。并以基于AWSIM/OpenSCENARIO的目标检测与制动场景开展案例研究。

Result: 案例显示BA-TRACE成功重构出预期的跨平台路径，并暴露出边界特有的延迟（如点云传输开销）。

Conclusion: BA-TRACE为混合AUTOSAR AP与ROS 2车载系统提供跨域可追踪性证据；重构拓扑仅作为可追踪性证据，而非行为正确性或安全性的证明。

Abstract: Modern vehicular embedded systems increasingly combine ROS 2-based autonomous-driving stacks with AUTOSAR Adaptive Platform (AUTOSAR AP). Such mixed stacks make scenario-based evaluation hard to interpret because execution paths cross DDS-SOME/IP middleware boundaries between ROS 2 and AUTOSAR AP. Existing simulators and tracing tools execute scenarios or collect platform-local traces but cannot reconstruct cross-domain data flows. This paper presents BA-TRACE, a boundary-aware trace reconstruction framework for scenario-based evaluation of mixed AUTOSAR AP and ROS 2 vehicular embedded systems. BA-TRACE combines ROS 2 trace events, AUTOSAR ara::log events, ARXML-derived structural dependencies, and bridge-level instrumentation to reconstruct an end-to-end execution graph across the DDS-SOME/IP boundary. A case study with an AWSIM/OpenSCENARIO-based object-detection and braking scenario shows that BA-TRACE reconstructs the expected cross-platform path and exposes boundary-specific latency such as point-cloud transfer overhead. The reconstructed topology is used as evidence of traceability, not as proof of behavioral correctness or safety.

</details>


### [395] [A Closed-Loop Control Architecture for Reliable Constraint Satisfaction in LLM Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19710)
*Quan Zhou,Shahbaz Siddeeq,Mika Saari,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出五阶段闭环控制架构（生成-评估-调整-归档-分析），让LLM只负责写/改文本，由确定性代码判定数值约束是否满足并做接受决策。在4个商用模型240次闭环运行中，达标率从单次提示的21.1-31.6%提升至92.5-98.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件系统越来越多地要求LLM输出满足数值约束（如字数目标、可读性等级区间），但LLM具有非确定性、仅通过自然语言指令配置、只能近似满足要求，单次提示既无法可靠达标也无法保留源内容。

Method: 闭环控制架构，含五个阶段：生成、评估、调整、归档、分析。模型仅用于撰写与编辑文本；确定性代码将复合可读性值对比目标区间，拒绝任何丢失源实体、数字或关键词的编辑，并做出所有接受决策。

Result: 114个单次生成任务与240次闭环运行，覆盖4个商用模型。单次提示达标率21.1%-31.6%，闭环达92.5%-98.8%，平均两轮编辑内完成，基于召回率的保真度0.92-0.93；两种同时参与两种设置的模型显示相同效应。

Conclusion: 由于控制器优化的正是评分所用的指标，该结果仅证明对声明式可计算指标的可靠控制，而非验证人类可读难度。可迁移的实践是：将接受条件声明为代码、限制模型只做局部编辑、并对每次编辑进行内容检查。

Abstract: Software systems increasingly embed a large language model in features that must satisfy a numeric output constraint, that is, a requirement expressible as a number or an interval and checkable by code, such as a target word count or a target readability grade band. Because such a model is non-deterministic, is configured through natural-language instructions rather than a typed interface, and satisfies a stated requirement only approximately, a single prompt neither reliably meets the target nor preserves the source content. This paper presents and evaluates a closed-loop control architecture for this problem. It has five stages: generate, evaluate, adjust, archive, and analyze. The model is called only to write and to edit text, while deterministic code compares a composite readability value against a target band, rejects any edit that drops source entities, numbers, or keywords, and makes every accept decision. Over 114 single-shot generation jobs and 240 closed-loop runs on four commercial models, single-shot prompting met the target in 21.1 to 31.6 percent of cases and the closed loop in 92.5 to 98.8 percent, within two edit rounds on average and at a recall-based fidelity of 0.92 to 0.93; the two models common to both settings show the same effect. Because the controller optimizes the value on which success is scored, the result establishes reproducible control over a declared, computable metric and not validated human difficulty. The transferable practice is to declare the acceptance condition as code, bound the model to local edits, and gate every edit on a content check.

</details>


### [396] [EviRCA: Decoupling Evidence Extraction from Reasoning for Microservice Root-Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19825)
*Yuhao Wang,Zhen Qin,Xingliang Wang,Guochang Li,Weize Li,Shuiguang Deng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EviRCA 将确定性证据提取与 LLM 推理解耦，在 OpenRCA 基准上正确率提升至 40.6%-43.9%，同时大幅降低 token 消耗和执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代微服务系统中根因分析（RCA）重要但耗费人力。现有 agentic 方法让单个 LLM 同时检索证据、定位故障并推断根因，需处理大量异构遥测数据，导致计算成本高、行为不稳定、诊断准确率有限；同时原始遥测（数值指标、结构化 trace、机器日志）不适合 LLM 直接处理。

Method: EviRCA 将确定性证据提取与 LLM 推理解耦。系统无关的提取阶段把原始 metrics、traces、logs 转换为紧凑且忠实可靠的多模态 evidence cards；LLM 仅通过少量预定义的只读工具对这些结构化观测进行推理，不接触原始遥测，也不执行代码。

Result: 在基于三个企业系统真实异构遥测构建的 OpenRCA 基准上，EviRCA 在两种不同 LLM 上取得 40.6%-43.9% 的正确率，远超此前最高 15.2% 的基线；token 消耗降低 15-26 倍，执行时间缩短 3-20 倍。此外，它能解决此前方法近乎零性能的、需同时推理时间、组件与根因的困难案例。

Conclusion: 过程级失败分析表明，瓶颈在于判断提取阶段已呈现的证据，而非搜索证据。这说明 LLM-based RCA 的效果在很大程度上取决于证据提取的质量。

Abstract: Root-cause analysis (RCA) is a critical yet labor-intensive task for maintaining modern microservice systems, making it an attractive target for large language models (LLMs). Recent agentic approaches allow an LLM to iteratively explore raw telemetry by generating and executing code, asking a single model to simultaneously retrieve evidence, localize faults, and infer root causes over large volumes of heterogeneous telemetry, which leads to high computational cost, unstable behavior, and limited diagnostic accuracy. However, raw telemetry consists of numeric metrics, structured traces, and machine-generated logs that are not directly suitable for LLM processing. We present EviRCA, a framework for LLM-based RCA that decouples deterministic evidence extraction from LLM reasoning. A system-agnostic extraction stage converts raw metrics, traces, and logs into a compact set of faithful multimodal evidence cards, while the LLM reasons only over these structured observations through a small set of predefined read-only tools, without accessing raw telemetry or executing code. We evaluate EviRCA on OpenRCA, a benchmark built from real, heterogeneous telemetry across three enterprise systems. EviRCA achieves a correct rate of 40.6%-43.9% across two different LLMs, substantially outperforming prior OpenRCA baselines that achieve up to 15.2%, while reducing token consumption by 15-26x and execution time by 3-20x. Moreover, EviRCA solves hard cases requiring simultaneous reasoning over time, components, and root causes, a setting where previous approaches reported near-zero performance. Our process-level failure analysis further shows that the bottleneck lies in judging the evidence that the extraction stage has already surfaced, rather than searching for it, suggesting that the effectiveness of LLM-based RCA depends heavily on the quality of evidence extraction.

</details>


### [397] [HapCiD: Detecting API-related Compatibility Issues in OpenHarmony Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20099)
*Daihang Chen,Yonghui Liu,Mingyi Zhou,Li Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出HapCiD，一个自动检测OpenHarmony应用中API兼容性问题的开源工具，在4478个应用上检测出2040个问题，准确率100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenHarmony作为新兴开源移动平台，其快速演进的SDK不断引入大量API，导致兼容性问题。现有兼容性分析工具因ArkTS新语言及缺乏成熟行为模型而无法有效适用于OpenHarmony。

Method: 提出HapCiD，通过建模应用执行语义自动检测OpenHarmony应用中的API相关兼容性问题，并将其应用于4478个应用进行实证研究。

Result: 在4478个应用中，于163个应用内成功检测出2040个兼容性问题，准确率达100%。

Conclusion: 基于检测结果对兼容性问题进行了系统研究，对其类型进行分类，考察现有缓解策略并提出未来改进方向。

Abstract: OpenHarmony, an emerging open-source mobile platform, is rapidly gaining attention in the mobile development community. Its fast-evolving Software Development Kit (SDK) introduces numerous Application Programming Interfaces (APIs) to boost developer productivity but inevitably introduces compatibility challenges, an issue well known from platforms like Android. However, existing compatibility analysis tools are ineffective for OpenHarmony due to its newly introduced ArkTS programming language and the lack of a mature behavioral model to capture app execution semantics. To bridge this gap, we present HapCiD, an open-source tool for automatically detecting API-related compatibility issues in OpenHarmony apps. Applied to 4,478 apps, HapCiD successfully detects 2,040 compatibility issues across 163 apps with 100 percent accuracy. Based on these findings, we conduct a comprehensive study of compatibility issues, categorizing their types, examining existing mitigation strategies, and proposing future improvements.

</details>


### [398] [AdaRepair-Mem: Adaptive Experience Orchestration for Repository-Level Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20130)
*Z. C. Luo,J. C. Guo,W. J. He,S. Y. Wang,J. C. Yu,F. M. Zhao,Y. Chen,T. Cao,L. Q. Liu,N. Zheng,W. Xu,J. Jiang,Z. M. Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文针对仓库级程序修复中记忆检索的三个缺陷，提出自适应经验检索框架，通过覆盖感知检索、质量感知选择和阶段感知路由提升修复效果，在SWE-Bench上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强的仓库级程序修复依赖复用历史修复经验，但存在三大局限：1) 情节记忆在仓库间高度不平衡，低资源仓库缺乏支持；2) 记忆量增加并不单调提升修复成功率，相关性、质量和冗余度更关键；3) 记忆积累与阶段错位，如很多复现经验但缺少补丁或精炼经验。

Method: 提出自适应经验检索框架，包含三部分：覆盖感知检索（同仓库记忆不足时回退到跨仓库或修复类型记忆）、质量感知选择（按相关性、历史效用、具体性和冗余度排序）、阶段感知路由（针对复现、定位、补丁生成、补丁精炼、验证分别检索记忆）。

Result: 在SWE-Bench-Lite和SWE-Bench-Verified上评估，改善了覆盖不足仓库的修复性能，减少了噪声记忆检索，更好地支持失败到修复的补丁精炼。

Conclusion: 记忆增强修复的关键不是简单积累更多经验，而是为正确的修复情境检索正确的经验。

Abstract: Recent memory-augmented repository-level program repair methods reuse historical repair experiences to improve LLM-based issue resolution. However, our analysis reveals three limitations in existing repository-level memory retrieval. First, episodic memory is highly imbalanced across repositories, leaving low-resource repositories with little effective support. Second, more memory does not monotonically lead to higher repair success, suggesting that relevance, quality, and redundancy matter more than raw memory volume. Third, memory accumulation is phase-misaligned: repositories may contain many reproduction experiences but few patch or refinement experiences. To address these problems, we propose an adaptive experience retrieval framework for repository-level program repair. Our framework introduces coverage-aware retrieval, which falls back to cross-repository or repair-type-based memories when same-repository memory is insufficient; quality-aware selection, which ranks memories by relevance, historical utility, specificity, and redundancy; and stage-aware routing, which separates and retrieves memories for reproduction, localization, patch generation, patch refinement, and validation. Evaluated on SWE-Bench-Lite and SWE-Bench-Verified, the proposed framework improves repair performance on under-covered repositories, reduces noisy memory retrieval, and better supports failed-to-fixed patch refinement. Our results show that the key to memory-augmented repair is not simply accumulating more experiences, but retrieving the right experiences for the right repair context.

</details>


### [399] [CodeTransBenchmark: Evaluating LLM-based Code Translation and Repair Across Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20257)
*Vera Kowalczuk,Oliver Weißl,Severin Kacianka,Andrea Stocco*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 CodeTransBenchmark 框架，系统评估大语言模型（LLM）在代码翻译与翻译错误修复上的能力，发现专用多语言模型（如 Codestral）表现较好，而通用模型易受目标语言语法规则困扰，并证明迭代式自动反馈修复能显著提升翻译准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在代码生成上展现潜力，吸引其在代码翻译领域的关注，但其翻译效果及翻译错误修复的有效性尚缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 提出 CodeTransBenchmark 评估框架，并设计后处理策略从 LLM 不一致输出中提取代码；在 3 个数据集、12 个语言对、8 个模型上开展实证研究，对错误翻译按错误类型分类，并研究基于自动反馈的迭代式翻译修复。

Result: 多语言专用模型（如 Codestral）可正确翻译大部分代码，而多数通用模型难以掌握目标语言语法；错误翻译分析表明编程语言间关系与训练数据对效果影响显著；通用后处理需容忍不一致并利用 LLM 答案的可预测性；迭代修复显著提升翻译准确率。

Conclusion: LLM 有望自动化代码翻译，但实际部署需要具备更大上下文窗口的模型。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) pre-trained on expansive text and code corpora have revealed promising code generation abilities and have attracted increasing attention in code translation. In this work, we investigate the effectiveness of LLMs in code translation and translation error repair. First, we present CodeTransBenchmark, a framework for evaluating LLM-based translation and repair and devise a post-processing strategy to extract code from inconsistent LLM outputs. Then, we discuss an empirical study evaluates eight models on three datasets and 12 language pairs, in which we categorize incorrect translations by errors to identify weaknesses of existing LLMs. Our work shows that while LLMs specifically trained for multi-lingual coding, like Codestral, correctly translate the majority of code, most general-purpose models struggle with the syntactic rules of the target language. The analysis of erroneous translations reveals the substantial impact of the interrelationship between involved programming languages and training data on the effectiveness. We show that a general post-processing approach must tolerate inconsistencies and leverage the predictability of LLM answers. Further, we show that iterative translation repair via automated feedback significantly improves translation accuracy. While our combined findings highlight the potential of LLMs to automate code translation, an effective deployment of LLM-based code translation in practice would require models with larger context windows.

</details>


### [400] [Evaluating Package-Level Scoping Strategies for Repository-Level Code Completion in Pharo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20279)
*Omar Abedelkader,Stéphane Ducasse,Guillermo Polito,Oleksandr Zaitsev,Romain Robbes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文为Pharo代码补全引擎引入三种包感知启发式策略，其中"直接包依赖"效果最佳，显著提升类名与方法名补全的MRR，并已被Pharo 13采用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token级补全引擎很少利用代码的仓库/包/项目结构；Pharo默认补全策略只遵循语言语义与作用域规则，忽略了包结构和依赖关系，导致同包或同项目类、以及被其使用的类得不到优先排序。

Method: 提出并评估三种包感知启发式：横向包（Lateral Packages）、直接包依赖（Direct Package Dependencies）、两级包依赖（Two-Level Package Dependencies）。基准覆盖219个包的方法调用点、4,535个类、35,972个方法，来自Iceberg、Moose、Roassal、Seaside、Spec等主要Pharo项目，评估类名与方法名补全，不引入新学习模型。

Result: 直接包依赖启发式效果最强。对3字符前缀，类名补全MRR从0.20提升至0.44，方法名补全从0.10提升至0.41。

Conclusion: 包感知策略在评估项目上比默认基于语义的策略提供更准确、更相关的建议；基于类名补全的提升，Pharo工业联盟已将直接包依赖启发式应用于Pharo 13。

Abstract: Source code is organized in repositories, packages, and projects, yet token-level completion engines rarely exploit this structure. Pharo provides a semantic completion engine based on ordered, lazy heuristics that retrieve and rank candidates. Its default strategy follows language semantics and scoping rules, but ignores package structure and dependencies. Consequently, classes from the same package or project, and classes used by them, receive no special priority.
  We introduce and evaluate three package-aware heuristics: Lateral Packages, Direct Package Dependencies, and Two-Level Package Dependencies. Our benchmark covers method call sites from 219 packages, including 4,535 classes and 35,972 methods across major Pharo projects such as Iceberg, Moose, Roassal, Seaside, and Spec. We assess class-name and method-name completion and ask whether explicit package dependencies can provide a lightweight structural signal for improving candidate ranking without introducing a new learning model.
  The Direct Package Dependencies heuristic produced the strongest results. For 3-character prefixes, Mean Reciprocal Rank increased from 0.20 to 0.44 for class-name completion and from 0.10 to 0.41 for method-name completion. These findings show that package-aware strategies provide more accurate and relevant suggestions than the default Semantics-Based strategy on the evaluated projects. Based on the improvement in class-name completion, the Pharo Industrial Consortium adopted the Direct Package Dependencies heuristic for Pharo 13.

</details>


### [401] [Towards a Characterization of Microservice Architectures Generated by Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20308)
*José Renan,Ademar Sousa,Emanuel Dantas,Danyllo Albuquerque,Mirko Perkusic,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文实证研究LLM从模块化单体自然语言描述生成微服务架构的行为，发现提示策略（zero-shot vs few-shot）比模型来源对生成架构结构影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多用于软件设计，但其生成的架构结构一致性与在"架构到架构"转换中的行为理解不足，尤其缺乏实证基线。

Method: 对两个模块化单体系统做受控实验，比较zero-shot与few-shot提示，使用OpenAI GPT类模型和DeepSeek模型；生成架构标准化为CSV格式，用归一化指标评估服务粒度、服务间通信模式、通信密度、服务隔离度和职责描述详尽度。

Result: 差异主要源自提示策略而非模型厂商。few-shot提示一致产生更细粒度划分、更低通信密度和更详细的职责描述；zero-shot倾向更粗粒度和更紧密连接的架构。

Conclusion: 本研究不评估架构质量，而是为理解文本驱动的架构到架构转换中LLM行为提供实证基线，为生成式设计工作流和早期软件现代化中的混合架构推理研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for software design tasks, yet little is known about the structure and coherence of the architectures they produce. In particular, how LLMs behave in Architecture-to-Architecture transformations remains insufficiently understood. This study empirically characterizes how LLMs generate microservice architectures from natural language descriptions of modular monoliths, focusing on structural and descriptive properties rather than architectural correctness. We conduct controlled experiments on two modular monolith systems, comparing zero-shot and few-shot prompting strategies using GPT-based models from OpenAI and models from DeepSeek. Generated architectures are represented in a standardized CSV format and evaluated using normalized metrics capturing service granularity, inter-service communication patterns, communication density, service isolation, and responsibility descriptiveness. The results reveal systematic differences driven primarily by prompting strategy rather than model provider. Few-shot prompting consistently produces finer-grained decompositions with lower communication density and more detailed responsibility descriptions, whereas zero-shot prompting favors coarser and more tightly connected architectures. Rather than assessing architectural quality, this study provides an empirical baseline for understanding LLM behavior in text-driven Architecture-to-Architecture transformations, informing future research on generative design workflows and hybrid architectural reasoning in early-stage software modernization.

</details>


### [402] [Assessing the Construct Validity of Object-Oriented, Class-Level Code Quality Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20411)
*Hera Arif,Miikka Kuutila,Paul Ralph*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文用现代测量理论（EFA+CFA）检验面向对象代码质量度量的构念效度，发现24个度量对应6个构念，其中耦合与继承是多维构念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已提出并使用大量代码质量度量，但其构念效度鲜有评估，导致这些度量究竟测量了什么往往不清楚。

Method: 从Apache Maven项目用Designite、JHawk、Understand三个工具提取类级面向对象度量，先用探索性因子分析（EFA）识别因子结构，再在22个符合条件的随机开源项目上用验证性因子分析（CFA）验证。

Result: EFA揭示24个度量对应6个构念：内聚、入耦合、出耦合、规模、子继承、父继承；10个度量不对应任何已知质量维度被移除，另有10个度量在CFA中载荷偏低。最终保留规模、内聚、继承、耦合四大构念，继承与耦合分出子类。

Conclusion: 结果有力支持24个代码质量度量的构念效度；耦合与继承是多维构念，需测量两个不同概念；复杂度或更适合用多层模型探索。研究展示了现代测量理论与潜变量建模在验证代码质量度量中的价值。

Abstract: Background: Code quality metrics are intended to measure latent properties of software source code. Although numerous code metrics have been proposed and used, their construct validity is rarely evaluated. Thus, the extent to which code metrics actually measure what they claim to measure is often unclear. Aim: Drawing from modern measurement theory, we investigate the construct validity of common class-level, object-oriented code quality metrics by identifying their factor structure using Exploratory Factor Analysis (EFA). The metrics were extracted from the Apache Maven project by three software tools: Designite, JHawk, and Understand. The factor structure was later verified using Confirmatory Factor Analysis (CFA) on 22 randomly selected open source projects meeting a predetermined eligibility criteria. Results: 24 code quality metrics that correspond to six constructs: Cohesion, In-Coupling, Out-Coupling, Size, Sub-Inheritance (related to subclasses), and Sup-Inheritance (related to superclasses) were revealed in the underlying factor structure. Ten metrics did not correspond to any known dimension of software quality and were removed in the EFA. Ten additional metrics exhibited low loadings in the CFA, suggesting their removal from the final measurement model. Size, Cohesion, Inheritance, and Coupling were the constructs retained, with subcategories identified for Inheritance and Coupling. Conclusions: Our results strongly support the construct validity of 24 code quality metrics. Coupling and Inheritance are revealed as multidimensional constructs, since they require measuring two different concepts, revealed as sub-categories in our analysis, and Complexity may be better explored in a multilevel model. Overall, our study demonstrates the value of applying modern measurement theory and latent variable modeling in validating software code quality metrics.

</details>


### [403] [Spotlights: Discovering Improvement Opportunities in Software Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20446)
